## Psalms 1:1 (id: 10019001001)

1The person who will have a truly good life is the person who does not do what evil people tell him to do, who does not imitate sinful people’s behavior, and who does not become like people who enjoy mocking.


## Psalms 1:2 (id: 10019001002)

2Instead, he delights in what Yahweh teaches, he thoughtfully reads it aloud every day and every night.


## Psalms 1:3 (id: 10019001003)

3Like having plenty of water causes a well\-cultivated tree to produce fruit at the right time, and keeps it from dying, so {his faithfulness to Yahweh} causes everything this person does to prosper.


## Psalms 1:4 (id: 10019001004)

4That is not what wicked people are like! Instead, they are like chaff that the wind blows away.


## Psalms 1:5 (id: 10019001005)

## Psalms 1:6 (id: 10019001006)

6{All of this is} because Yahweh cares for righteous people, but wicked people keep sinning until {Yahweh} destroys them {forever}.

## Psalms 2:7 (id: 10019002007)

## Psalms 2:8 (id: 10019002008)

## Psalms 2:9 (id: 10019002009)

## Psalms 2:10 (id: 10019002010)

## Psalms 2:11 (id: 10019002011)

## Psalms 2:12 (id: 10019002012)

## Psalms 4:8 (id: 10019004008)

## Psalms 5:12 (id: 10019005012)

## Psalms 7:16 (id: 10019007016)

## Psalms 7:17 (id: 10019007017)

## Psalms 10:1 (id: 10019010001)

1Yahweh, please do not stay far away from us. Please let us find you when enemies threaten us.


## Psalms 10:2 (id: 10019010002)

2Wicked people proudly chase people until they can oppress them. Yahweh, please make the bad things they plan to do to others happen to them instead.


## Psalms 10:3 (id: 10019010003)

3A wicked person even brags about what he strongly wants to do. He praises people who unjustly take things from other people. He curses Yahweh.


## Psalms 10:4 (id: 10019010004)

4The wicked person is so proud that he does not care {about God}. He plans {to do evil things} as if there was no God.


## Psalms 10:5 (id: 10019010005)

5What he does always prospers. He ignores doing what you say is right. He makes a mocking noise at all his enemies.


## Psalms 10:6 (id: 10019010006)

## Psalms 10:7 (id: 10019010007)

## Psalms 10:8 (id: 10019010008)

## Psalms 10:9 (id: 10019010009)

## Psalms 10:10 (id: 10019010010)

10The wicked person makes sure everything is properly prepared so he can oppress his unlucky victim.


## Psalms 10:11 (id: 10019010011)

## Psalms 10:12 (id: 10019010012)

## Psalms 10:13 (id: 10019010013)

## Psalms 10:14 (id: 10019010014)

## Psalms 10:15 (id: 10019010015)

15Cause people who are always doing very evil things to become powerless! When someone tries to oppress other people may he discover he cannot do it.


## Psalms 10:16 (id: 10019010016)

16Yahweh reigns eternally! Foreign people no longer occupy his land.


## Psalms 10:17 (id: 10019010017)

17Yahweh, when oppressed people ask you to help, you listen and encourage them. You pay close attention,


## Psalms 10:18 (id: 10019010018)

18so you can act justly for the orphans and oppressed people. You will stop any mere human from ever terrifying them again.

## Psalms 15:1 (id: 10019015001)

## Psalms 16:1 (id: 10019016001)

1A song of David. God, protect me, because I go to you to keep me safe!

## Psalms 16:7 (id: 10019016007)

## Psalms 16:8 (id: 10019016008)

## Psalms 16:9 (id: 10019016009)

## Psalms 16:10 (id: 10019016010)

## Psalms 16:11 (id: 10019016011)

## Psalms 17:1 (id: 10019017001)

1A prayer of David Yahweh, listen to me while I plead with you to act justly toward me. Hear me while I call out to you to help me. Pay attention to what I say while I pray because I am speaking honestly.

## Psalms 17:4 (id: 10019017004)

## Psalms 17:5 (id: 10019017005)

## Psalms 17:6 (id: 10019017006)

## Psalms 17:7 (id: 10019017007)

## Psalms 17:9 (id: 10019017009)

## Psalms 17:14 (id: 10019017014)

## Psalms 18:0 (id: 10019018000)

I am as safe as I would be if I were hiding on top of a cliff. It is as if you are a shield that defends me. You are the powerful God who saves me. You are like a high place {where I am safe from my enemies}.

## Psalms 18:39 (id: 10019018039)

## Psalms 18:48 (id: 10019018048)

## Psalms 18:49 (id: 10019018049)

1A psalm of David Yahweh {cares for} me {like} a shepherd {cares for his sheep}, {so} I {know that I} will have everything that I need.

2Yahweh nourishes and encourages my inner person and enables me to rest in peace, like a shepherd who leads {his sheep} to places where there is plenty of green grass {for them to eat} and leads them to streams where the water is flowing slowly.

3Yahweh renews my strength. He gives me good guidance for how I should live, in order that I, and others, will honor him.

I feel comforted and reassured because you protect and care for me like a shepherd protects his sheep by using his club and his walking stick to protect them from being attacked by wild animals.

9Open up the gates {of the sacred tent}, in order that our glorious king may enter!


## Psalms 24:10 (id: 10019024010)

## Psalms 25:1 (id: 10019025001)

1A psalm of David Yahweh, I come to you in prayer and tell you all that I need.


## Psalms 25:2 (id: 10019025002)

2I trust in you, my God. Do not allow my enemies {to defeat me}, with the result that they would rejoice and I would be ashamed.


## Psalms 25:3 (id: 10019025003)

3Truly, you will not allow any of those who trust in you to be ashamed. You will cause those who act treacherously toward others to be ashamed.


## Psalms 25:4 (id: 10019025004)

4Yahweh, show me how I should conduct my life as you want me to. Teach me how to act in the manner that is pleasing to you.


## Psalms 25:5 (id: 10019025005)

5Teach me to conduct my life by obeying your truth, because you are my God, the one who saves me. I trust in you at all times.


## Psalms 25:6 (id: 10019025006)

6Yahweh, please act mercifully to me and faithfully love me, because that is the way that you have acted since long ago.


## Psalms 25:7 (id: 10019025007)

7Forgive me for the sinful things I did when I was young and for my rebellious actions {as an adult}. I ask that, because you faithfully love your people, you treat me kindly and forgive me. Yahweh, I ask that you do this because you are good!


## Psalms 25:8 (id: 10019025008)

8Yahweh is good and acts righteously, so he shows sinful people the right way to conduct their lives.


## Psalms 25:9 (id: 10019025009)

## Psalms 25:10 (id: 10019025010)

## Psalms 25:11 (id: 10019025011)

11Yahweh, forgive me for all my sins, which are many, in order that I and others may honor you.


## Psalms 25:12 (id: 10019025012)

12To all those who revere Yahweh, he shows them the right way to conduct their lives.


## Psalms 25:13 (id: 10019025013)

13They will always be prosperous, and their descendants will continue to live in {this} land.


## Psalms 25:14 (id: 10019025014)

14Yahweh is a friend of those who revere him, and he teaches them the covenant that he made {with them}.


## Psalms 25:15 (id: 10019025015)

## Psalms 25:16 (id: 10019025016)

16{Yahweh,} pay attention to me and be merciful to me, because I have no one to help me and I am suffering.


## Psalms 25:17 (id: 10019025017)

17{Because of my present circumstances} there are many things that cause me to worry. Rescue me from the things that are causing me to be distressed.


## Psalms 25:18 (id: 10019025018)

18Take notice that I am in difficult circumstances and troubled, and forgive me for every sinful thing I have done.


## Psalms 25:19 (id: 10019025019)

19Take notice that I have many enemies, and that they hate me very much.


## Psalms 25:20 (id: 10019025020)

## Psalms 25:21 (id: 10019025021)

21Protect me because I do what is good and honest, and because I trust in you.


## Psalms 25:22 (id: 10019025022)

## Psalms 26:1 (id: 10019026001)

1A psalm of David Yahweh, show that I am innocent. I have done what is right, and I have trusted in Yahweh without doubting him.


## Psalms 26:2 (id: 10019026002)

2Yahweh, examine what I have done and test me; thoroughly evaluate what I think in my inner being.


## Psalms 26:3 (id: 10019026003)

3I never forget that you faithfully love me. I conduct my life according to what you say is right.


## Psalms 26:4 (id: 10019026004)

## Psalms 26:5 (id: 10019026005)

5I do not like to be with evil people, and I avoid wicked people.


## Psalms 26:6 (id: 10019026006)

6Yahweh, I wash my hands to show that I have not done what was wrong. As I join with others marching around your altar,


## Psalms 26:7 (id: 10019026007)

7we sing songs to thank you, and we tell others the wonderful things that you have done.


## Psalms 26:8 (id: 10019026008)

8Yahweh, I love {to be in} the sanctuary where you dwell, in the place where your glory appears.


## Psalms 26:9 (id: 10019026009)

## Psalms 26:10 (id: 10019026010)

10and people who are ready to do wicked things, and people who are always taking bribes.


## Psalms 26:11 (id: 10019026011)

11But as for me, I always try to do what is right. So rescue me and act kindly toward me.


## Psalms 26:12 (id: 10019026012)

12{Because I have conducted myself righteously} I am secure, and when all Yahweh’s people gather together, I praise him among them.


## Psalms 27:1 (id: 10019027001)

1A psalm of David Yahweh encourages me and nourishes my inner being. He is the one who saves me, so I will not fear anyone. Yahweh is the one to whom I go for refuge, so I will not dread anyone.


## Psalms 27:2 (id: 10019027002)

2When those who do evil things come near me to attack and destroy me, Yahweh enables me to defeat them so that they fall down.


## Psalms 27:3 (id: 10019027003)

3Even if an army sets up their tents in order to attack me, I will not be afraid. Even if they attack me, I will trust {in God}.


## Psalms 27:4 (id: 10019027004)

When I am in his sanctuary then I will ask him {for guidance}.

## Psalms 27:9 (id: 10019027009)

9I am your servant; do not be angry with me, or turn away from me. You have always helped me. You are the one who has saved me, so do not abandon me now.


## Psalms 27:10 (id: 10019027010)

10My father and mother have left me, but Yahweh will take care of me.


## Psalms 27:11 (id: 10019027011)

11Yahweh, teach me to do what you want me to do, and guide me so that I remain safe because I have enemies {who want to hurt me}.


## Psalms 27:12 (id: 10019027012)

12Do not allow my enemies to do to me what they want, because they say false things about me and want violent things to happen to me.


## Psalms 27:13 (id: 10019027013)

## Psalms 28:1 (id: 10019028001)

1A psalm of David Yahweh, I call out to you. You are like a huge rock {on top of which I am safe}. Do not refuse to answer me because if you refuse to answer me, I will soon be with those who are in their graves.


## Psalms 28:2 (id: 10019028002)

2Pay attention to me and respond when I call out for you to help me when I pray, lifting up my hands as I face your very holy place in your sacred tent.


## Psalms 28:3 (id: 10019028003)

3Do not get rid of me when you get rid of wicked people, those who do wicked deeds. Those people speak peacefully toward others, while in their inner beings, they hate them and devise evil plans.


## Psalms 28:4 (id: 10019028004)

4Punish those people in the way that they deserve for what they have done; punish them for their evil deeds.


## Psalms 28:5 (id: 10019028005)

5Yahweh will get rid of them permanently and not let them appear again. Yahweh {will do this} because these evil people do not pay attention to the righteous things he has taught his people and do not take notice that he always judges evil deeds.


## Psalms 28:6 (id: 10019028006)

6Praise Yahweh because he responded to me when I called out for him to help me.


## Psalms 28:7 (id: 10019028007)

7Yahweh strengthens me and he protects me like a shield; I trust in him, and he helps me. As a result I am glad in my inner being, and I will praise him with my songs {of praise}.


## Psalms 28:8 (id: 10019028008)

8Yahweh strengthens and protects his people. Yahweh saves the one he appointed to be king.


## Psalms 28:9 (id: 10019028009)

9Yahweh, rescue your people and bless those who belong to you. Take care of them like a shepherd takes care of his sheep; take care of them forever.

## Psalms 29:8 (id: 10019029008)

## Psalms 29:9 (id: 10019029009)

## Psalms 29:11 (id: 10019029011)

## Psalms 30:0 (id: 10019030000)

0David wrote this psalm to be sung at the dedication of the tabernacle.


## Psalms 30:1 (id: 10019030001)

1Yahweh, I praise you because you rescued me. You did not allow {me to die with the result that} my enemies could gloat.


## Psalms 30:2 (id: 10019030002)

2Yahweh my God, I called out for you to help me {when I was sick,} you healed me.


## Psalms 30:3 (id: 10019030003)

3Yahweh, when I was dying you restored me. I was nearly dead, but you caused me to get well.


## Psalms 30:4 (id: 10019030004)

4All you who are faithful to Yahweh, sing praise to him. Think about what Yahweh, the holy one, has done, and thank him.


## Psalms 30:5 (id: 10019030005)

5When he becomes angry, he is angry for only a very short time, but he is good {to us} all of our lives. We may cry during the night, but the next morning we will be joyful.


## Psalms 30:6 (id: 10019030006)

6As for me, when my life was going well, I said {to myself}, “Nothing will ever trouble me.”


## Psalms 30:7 (id: 10019030007)

7Yahweh, because you were good to me, at first you caused me to be secure as a mountain. {But then you let me become ill when} you stopped protecting me {from illness}, and I became greatly disturbed.


## Psalms 30:8 (id: 10019030008)

8{Then} I called out to you, Yahweh, and I pleaded for you, the Lord, to help me.


## Psalms 30:9 (id: 10019030009)

9{I said,} “{Yahweh,} you will gain nothing if I die. It will not benefit you at all if I go to the place where the dead people are. Dead people certainly are not able to praise you, and they are not able to tell others that you are faithful.


## Psalms 30:10 (id: 10019030010)

10Yahweh, listen to me, and act mercifully to me. Yahweh, help me.”


## Psalms 30:11 (id: 10019030011)

## Psalms 30:12 (id: 10019030012)

12{You did that} so that I would not be silent but would sing out to praise you. Yahweh, you are my God, and I will thank you forever.

## Psalms 31:8 (id: 10019031008)

## Psalms 31:9 (id: 10019031009)

## Psalms 31:10 (id: 10019031010)

## Psalms 31:11 (id: 10019031011)

## Psalms 31:14 (id: 10019031014)

## Psalms 31:15 (id: 10019031015)

## Psalms 31:16 (id: 10019031016)

## Psalms 31:17 (id: 10019031017)

17Yahweh, I call out to you, so do not allow others to shame me. I desire that you cause wicked people to be ashamed. I desire that they {soon die and} go down to the place where the dead people are.


## Psalms 31:18 (id: 10019031018)

18I desire that you will cause people who tell lies to be unable to speak. {Do that to} people who arrogantly say disrespectful things about righteous people.


## Psalms 31:19 (id: 10019031019)

## Psalms 31:20 (id: 10019031020)

20You protect people {who trust in you} by being near to them and saving them from people who devise evil schemes {to harm them}. You keep them safe from their enemies who accuse them.


## Psalms 31:21 (id: 10019031021)

21Praise Yahweh! When my enemies surrounded the city {in which I was living,} he showed me in a wonderful way that he faithfully loves me.


## Psalms 31:22 (id: 10019031022)

## Psalms 31:23 (id: 10019031023)

## Psalms 31:24 (id: 10019031024)

## Psalms 32:1 (id: 10019032001)

1A song of David Those whom God has forgiven for rebelling against him, and those whose sin God has atoned for, these are the ones who will have a truly good life!


## Psalms 32:2 (id: 10019032002)

2Those whose record of sins Yahweh no longer takes into account, and those who are not deceitful, these are the ones who will have a truly good life!


## Psalms 32:3 (id: 10019032003)

3When I did not confess my sins, my body felt weak and sick, and I groaned all day long.


## Psalms 32:4 (id: 10019032004)

4Day and night, Yahweh, you afflicted me. My strength was sapped like it would be on a hot summer day. Selah


## Psalms 32:5 (id: 10019032005)

5{Then} I confessed my sinful actions to you; I stopped trying to hide them. I said to myself, “I will tell Yahweh the wrong things that I have done.” When I confessed them, you forgave me, so now I am no longer guilty for the sinful things I did. Selah


## Psalms 32:6 (id: 10019032006)

6Therefore all the people who are godly should pray to you when they realize they have sinned. {If they do that,} then it is certain that difficulties will not overwhelm them like a great flood.


## Psalms 32:7 (id: 10019032007)

7You are like a place where I can hide from my enemies; you protect me from troubles and surround me with people who are joyfully praising you for saving me from my enemies. Selah


## Psalms 32:8 (id: 10019032008)

8I will teach you about how you should conduct your life. I will teach you personally.


## Psalms 32:9 (id: 10019032009)

## Psalms 32:10 (id: 10019032010)

10Wicked people will have many difficult things in their lives that will make them sad, but those who trust in Yahweh will experience him faithfully loving them all the time.


## Psalms 32:11 (id: 10019032011)

11{So, all} you righteous people, rejoice about {what} Yahweh {has done for you}! All you whose inner beings are pure, be glad and shout joyfully!


## Psalms 33:1 (id: 10019033001)

1You people who do what is right, shout joyfully because of Yahweh, because praising him is what upright people should do.


## Psalms 33:2 (id: 10019033002)

2Praise Yahweh by playing songs on the harp. Praise him as you make music for him with instruments that have many strings.


## Psalms 33:3 (id: 10019033003)

3Sing a new song to him; play those instruments well, and shout joyfully as you play them!


## Psalms 33:4 (id: 10019033004)

4Do this because everything that Yahweh says is true, and we can trust that everything that he does is right.


## Psalms 33:5 (id: 10019033005)

5He loves everything that people do that is right and just. Yahweh faithfully loves people all over the earth.


## Psalms 33:6 (id: 10019033006)

6Yahweh created everything in the sky by commanding it. He created all of the stars by speaking.


## Psalms 33:7 (id: 10019033007)

7He gathered all the water of the sea into one huge mass as though scooping it into a container.


## Psalms 33:8 (id: 10019033008)

8Everyone on the earth should deeply revere Yahweh.


## Psalms 33:9 (id: 10019033009)

9They should revere him because he is the one who spoke, making the world exist. Everything started to exist because he is the one who commanded it.


## Psalms 33:10 (id: 10019033010)

10Yahweh stops the nations from doing what they plan to do. He prevents the peoples from carrying out what they decide.


## Psalms 33:11 (id: 10019033011)

11However, what Yahweh decides to do will last forever. What he plans to do will never change.


## Psalms 33:12 (id: 10019033012)

12What a good life people have when Yahweh is the God of their nation; he chose those people to belong to him.


## Psalms 33:13 (id: 10019033013)

13Yahweh looks down from heaven; he sees all human beings.


## Psalms 33:14 (id: 10019033014)

14From the place where he lives he watches everyone who lives on the earth.


## Psalms 33:15 (id: 10019033015)

15He created each person’s heart, and he knows everything that they do.


## Psalms 33:16 (id: 10019033016)

16It is not because a king has a great army that he is able to win {battles}, and it is not because a soldier is very strong that he is able to defeat {his enemy}.


## Psalms 33:17 (id: 10019033017)

17It is foolish to hope that a horse can save anyone; even with its great strength, it cannot rescue {its rider}.


## Psalms 33:18 (id: 10019033018)

18Note this: Yahweh watches over those who deeply revere him; He watches over those who confidently expect him to faithfully love them,


## Psalms 33:19 (id: 10019033019)

19to save them from dying and to preserve them alive through any famine.


## Psalms 33:20 (id: 10019033020)

20We trust that Yahweh will take care of us; he will help us and protect us.


## Psalms 33:21 (id: 10019033021)

21Indeed, he makes us very joyful; we trust him because he is holy.


## Psalms 33:22 (id: 10019033022)

22Yahweh, we pray that you will always faithfully love us, while we confidently wait for you {to help us}.


## Psalms 34:0 (id: 10019034000)

## Psalms 34:2 (id: 10019034002)

## Psalms 34:3 (id: 10019034003)

## Psalms 34:4 (id: 10019034004)

4I prayed to Yahweh, and he answered me; he rescued me from everything that I was afraid of.


## Psalms 34:5 (id: 10019034005)

5Those who ask him {to help them} will be joyful; may Yahweh always provide for them.


## Psalms 34:6 (id: 10019034006)

## Psalms 34:7 (id: 10019034007)

7An angel representing Yahweh guards people who deeply revere Yahweh, and the angel rescues them {from bad things}.


## Psalms 34:8 (id: 10019034008)

## Psalms 34:9 (id: 10019034009)

## Psalms 34:10 (id: 10019034010)

## Psalms 34:11 (id: 10019034011)

## Psalms 34:12 (id: 10019034012)

12If any of you want to truly live and to enjoy a good, long life,


## Psalms 34:13 (id: 10019034013)

## Psalms 34:14 (id: 10019034014)

14Stop doing what is evil and instead do what is good; always strive to live peacefully with others.


## Psalms 34:15 (id: 10019034015)

15Yahweh pays attention to people who do what is right, and he listens when they call {to him to help them}.


## Psalms 34:16 (id: 10019034016)

16But Yahweh opposes people who do what is evil. He does not allow their descendants to remain on the earth.


## Psalms 34:17 (id: 10019034017)

## Psalms 34:18 (id: 10019034018)

18Yahweh is always ready to help people who are very sad, and he saves people who are deeply discouraged.


## Psalms 34:19 (id: 10019034019)

## Psalms 34:20 (id: 10019034020)

## Psalms 34:21 (id: 10019034021)

## Psalms 34:22 (id: 10019034022)

## Psalms 35:1 (id: 10019035001)

1A psalm of David. Yahweh, be the enemy of my enemies! Defend me against those who attack me!


## Psalms 35:2 (id: 10019035002)

2Protect me like a soldier who has both of his shields, and come to help me!


## Psalms 35:3 (id: 10019035003)

3Take your spear and javelin and confront those who are pursuing me! Tell me, “I am the one who saves you.”


## Psalms 35:4 (id: 10019035004)

4Please defeat and dishonor those who want to kill me, and make those who plan to harm me become confused and turn back.


## Psalms 35:5 (id: 10019035005)

5May an angel from Yahweh chase them away so that they disappear like chaff in the wind.


## Psalms 35:6 (id: 10019035006)

6May their lives be like running on a path that is dark and slippery, while an angel from Yahweh pursues them!


## Psalms 35:7 (id: 10019035007)

7Please do these things to them, because I have not done anything wrong to them, yet they try to kill me. It is as though they hide nets and dig pits for me to walk into!


## Psalms 35:8 (id: 10019035008)

8So let disaster suddenly surprise each of them! Let it be as though the nets that they hid catch them instead, and as though they fall into their own {pits} and die!


## Psalms 35:9 (id: 10019035009)

9Then I will rejoice because of {what} Yahweh {has done for me}, and I will be glad because he has saved me.


## Psalms 35:10 (id: 10019035010)

10With my whole being I will say, “Yahweh, no one is like you! No one else rescues weak people from those who are stronger, no one else rescues poor and needy people from those who rob them.”


## Psalms 35:11 (id: 10019035011)

11Hostile people tell lies {about me} in court and accuse me of things that I know nothing about.


## Psalms 35:12 (id: 10019035012)

12I was good to them. But instead of being good to me, they are evil to me. That makes me feel completely alone.


## Psalms 35:13 (id: 10019035013)

13Yet for my part, when they were sick, I put on rough cloth to mourn for them; I went without food as I prayed for them, bowing my head over my chest.


## Psalms 35:14 (id: 10019035014)

14I walked around grieving for them as I would grieve for a friend or for a brother, and I bowed down mourning for them as though for my own mother.


## Psalms 35:15 (id: 10019035015)

15But when I suffered, they all gathered together and celebrated. People whom I did not even know gathered to attack me. They kept slandering me and would not stop.


## Psalms 35:16 (id: 10019035016)

16Profane people mocked me for being lame and ground their teeth at me {to show how angry they were}.


## Psalms 35:17 (id: 10019035017)

17Lord, please do more than just watch what they do! Rescue me from these people who want to destroy me! Save me from these people who are as fierce as young lions!


## Psalms 35:18 (id: 10019035018)

18Then I will thank you when the great crowd of your people gather together, and I will praise you in front of all of them.


## Psalms 35:19 (id: 10019035019)

19Do not let my enemies who lie about me celebrate {because they defeated me}, and do not let those who hate me for no reason wink at each other {to show how pleased they are about it}.


## Psalms 35:20 (id: 10019035020)

20{Do not let them succeed,} because they do not speak peacefully {to anyone}; instead, they make up lies about quiet, peaceable people in the land.


## Psalms 35:21 (id: 10019035021)

21They shout loudly to accuse me, saying, “Yes! Yes! We saw {what you did}!”


## Psalms 35:22 (id: 10019035022)

22Yahweh, you have seen all of this, so please do something about it! Lord, please help me!


## Psalms 35:23 (id: 10019035023)

23Become active and do the right thing for me; my God and my Lord, defend me!


## Psalms 35:24 (id: 10019035024)

24Yahweh my God, because you are righteous, declare that I am innocent, and do not let my enemies celebrate {that they have defeated} me.


## Psalms 35:25 (id: 10019035025)

25Do not let them think to themselves, “Yes! We got what we wanted!” Do not let them say {to each other}, “We have completely destroyed him!”


## Psalms 35:26 (id: 10019035026)

26Instead, make all those who celebrate when bad things happen to me feel totally ashamed and disgraced. Yes, completely humiliate those who boast that they are greater than me.


## Psalms 35:27 (id: 10019035027)

27But let those who want God to show that I am innocent shout for joy and celebrate! Let them always say, “Yahweh is great! He loves to make things go well for the one who serves him.”


## Psalms 35:28 (id: 10019035028)

28Then I will tell everyone how righteous you are, and I will praise you all day long.


## Psalms 36:0 (id: 10019036000)

1Wicked people act as though they are obeying a voice from deep within themselves that is urging them to sin. They have no respect for God at all.


## Psalms 36:2 (id: 10019036002)

2Indeed, they are so proud that they refuse to recognize how wrongly they act so that they might hate {doing those things}.


## Psalms 36:3 (id: 10019036003)

3Everything that they say is wicked and deceptive; they have stopped acting wisely and {stopped} doing what is right.

## Psalms 36:5 (id: 10019036005)

## Psalms 36:6 (id: 10019036006)

## Psalms 36:7 (id: 10019036007)

7God, how precious it is that you faithfully love us! You protect people just as a bird shelters its young under its wings.


## Psalms 36:8 (id: 10019036008)

## Psalms 36:9 (id: 10019036009)

## Psalms 36:10 (id: 10019036010)

## Psalms 36:11 (id: 10019036011)

11Do not let arrogant people overcome me, and do not let wicked people force me to flee.


## Psalms 36:12 (id: 10019036012)

## Psalms 37:1 (id: 10019037001)

1This is a psalm of David. Do not let what wicked people do upset you; do not wish that you had what people who do evil things have.


## Psalms 37:2 (id: 10019037002)

2{Do not worry about them} because they will soon disappear, just as grass dries up {in the sun} and green plants quickly wither and die.


## Psalms 37:3 (id: 10019037003)

## Psalms 37:5 (id: 10019037005)

## Psalms 37:6 (id: 10019037006)

6He will show everyone as clearly as the light of the noonday sun that you do what is right and that you are innocent of doing anything wrong.


## Psalms 37:7 (id: 10019037007)

## Psalms 37:8 (id: 10019037008)

8Stop being angry {about what wicked people do}; do not want to punish them yourself; that only leads to doing evil.


## Psalms 37:9 (id: 10019037009)

## Psalms 37:10 (id: 10019037010)

10Soon wicked people will disappear. You will look for them where they used to be, but they will be gone.


## Psalms 37:11 (id: 10019037011)

## Psalms 37:12 (id: 10019037012)

12Wicked people plan to harm people who do what is right. They grind their teeth at people who do what is right {to show how much they hate them}.


## Psalms 37:13 (id: 10019037013)

## Psalms 37:14 (id: 10019037014)

14Wicked people draw their swords and string their bows to kill people who are poor and helpless and to slaughter people who live honestly.


## Psalms 37:15 (id: 10019037015)

15But their swords will pierce their own hearts, and the Lord will break their bows.


## Psalms 37:16 (id: 10019037016)

16It is better to be a person who does what is right and to have only a few possessions than to have the abundant wealth of many wicked people.


## Psalms 37:17 (id: 10019037017)

17{This is so} because Yahweh will make wicked people unable to do what they want to do, but he will take care of people who do what is right.


## Psalms 37:18 (id: 10019037018)

18Each day Yahweh watches over people who do not do wrong things, and what he gives them will last forever.


## Psalms 37:19 (id: 10019037019)

19During times when bad things happen they will not despair; even when there is a famine, they will still have enough {to eat}.


## Psalms 37:20 (id: 10019037020)

## Psalms 37:21 (id: 10019037021)

21Wicked people borrow {money} and never repay {it}, but people who do what is right are generous and give {freely to others}.


## Psalms 37:22 (id: 10019037022)

22Moreover, those whom Yahweh blesses will possess the land, but he will destroy those whom he has cursed.


## Psalms 37:23 (id: 10019037023)

23Yahweh directs a person to live securely, and he is pleased with how that person lives.


## Psalms 37:24 (id: 10019037024)

24Even if something bad happens to that person, he will not completely fail, because Yahweh takes care of him.


## Psalms 37:25 (id: 10019037025)

25I have been young, and now I am old, but {in all those years} I have never seen Yahweh stop caring for any person who does what is right, nor have I seen such a person’s children {needing to} beg for food.


## Psalms 37:26 (id: 10019037026)

## Psalms 37:27 (id: 10019037027)

## Psalms 37:28 (id: 10019037028)

## Psalms 37:29 (id: 10019037029)

29People who do what is right will possess the land {that Yahweh gives them} and will live there forever.

## Psalms 37:33 (id: 10019037033)

## Psalms 37:35 (id: 10019037035)

35I once saw a wicked and tyrannical person who was becoming powerful, like a large tree that was spreading out its branches over its native soil.


## Psalms 37:36 (id: 10019037036)

36But then he passed away! Suddenly, he was gone! I looked for him, but he was not there.


## Psalms 37:37 (id: 10019037037)

37Observe {and imitate} people who are honest and who live rightly, because people who live peacefully will have a long life.


## Psalms 37:38 (id: 10019037038)

38But Yahweh will destroy all people who rebel against him; he will make the lives of wicked people short.


## Psalms 37:39 (id: 10019037039)

39Yahweh saves people who do what is right; he protects them when bad things are happening.


## Psalms 37:40 (id: 10019037040)

## Psalms 38:0 (id: 10019038000)

0This is a psalm of David, to ask God to remember him.


## Psalms 38:1 (id: 10019038001)

1Yahweh, please do not correct me angrily; no, please do not punish me wrathfully.


## Psalms 38:2 (id: 10019038002)

2{I feel as though} you have shot arrows into me, and that you have struck me with your hand.


## Psalms 38:3 (id: 10019038003)

3Because you are so angry with me, my whole body suffers. My body is restless because I have sinned.


## Psalms 38:4 (id: 10019038004)

4Indeed, all my sins {are like flood waters that} rise above my head; they are like a heavy load that is too much for me to bear.


## Psalms 38:5 (id: 10019038005)

5Because I have acted foolishly, I have sores that keep getting worse and stink.


## Psalms 38:6 (id: 10019038006)

6My body is twisted and bent over very low. I go around sad and grieving constantly.


## Psalms 38:7 (id: 10019038007)

7This is because there is a fever inside me, and my whole body is sick.


## Psalms 38:8 (id: 10019038008)

8I am exhausted and feel completely broken. I moan loudly because I feel so distressed.


## Psalms 38:9 (id: 10019038009)

9Lord, you know everything that I long for, and you hear every time that I sigh.


## Psalms 38:10 (id: 10019038010)

10My heart beats rapidly and I am very weak. Even my eyesight is failing me.


## Psalms 38:11 (id: 10019038011)

11My friends and the people I love keep away from me because of my disease; even my own family stays far away from me.


## Psalms 38:12 (id: 10019038012)

12Those who want to kill me set traps for me, and those who want to harm me talk about how to destroy me and they constantly plot how they will deceive me.


## Psalms 38:13 (id: 10019038013)

13But I act like someone who is deaf, and I do not listen {to them}. I act like someone who cannot speak, and I say nothing {to them}.


## Psalms 38:14 (id: 10019038014)

14I have become like a man who cannot hear and who cannot argue back.


## Psalms 38:15 (id: 10019038015)

15{This is} because I wait for you, Yahweh{, to help me}. You will be the one who will respond, Lord my God.


## Psalms 38:16 (id: 10019038016)

16{I know this} because I prayed, “Do not let my enemies celebrate over me. Do not let them gloat over me if something bad happens to me.”


## Psalms 38:17 (id: 10019038017)

17Indeed, I feel that something bad will soon happen, and I am constantly in pain.


## Psalms 38:18 (id: 10019038018)

18Certainly, I confess all the wrong things that I have done; I feel terrible about my sin.


## Psalms 38:19 (id: 10019038019)

19But my vigorous enemies are powerful, and there are many people who hate me for no reason.


## Psalms 38:20 (id: 10019038020)

20When I do good things for people, some of them do evil things to me in return. Those people accuse me {of doing evil things}, even though I am trying to do what is right.


## Psalms 38:21 (id: 10019038021)

21Yahweh, please do not leave me! My God, please stay near me!


## Psalms 38:22 (id: 10019038022)

22Come quickly to help me, Lord, you who save me!


## Psalms 39:0 (id: 10019039000)

0This is a note to the worship leader: David wrote this song. Perform in the style of Jeduthun.


## Psalms 39:1 (id: 10019039001)

1I thought to myself, “I must be very careful about how I act so that I do not sin by what I say. I must keep myself from speaking while wicked people are near me.”


## Psalms 39:2 (id: 10019039002)

2So I kept completely silent; I did not say anything, not even anything good, but my suffering only became worse.


## Psalms 39:3 (id: 10019039003)

3I felt very agitated inside me; the more I pondered, the more agonized I became. Then finally I spoke and said,


## Psalms 39:4 (id: 10019039004)

4“Yahweh, let me know when I will die. Tell me how many days I have left to live. Help me to understand how short my life really is.


## Psalms 39:5 (id: 10019039005)

5Indeed, you have made my days very few; the time that I have to live is practically nothing to you. Truly, every person appears and disappears as quickly as vapor. Selah


## Psalms 39:6 (id: 10019039006)

6Truly, each person does not do anything more substantial than a shadow would do. Truly, people become agitated about things that do not matter. They pile up {wealth}, but they do not know who will get it {after they die}.


## Psalms 39:7 (id: 10019039007)

7So now, Lord, I realize that it is useless for me to expect any person to make my life purposeful. You are the only one who can do anything worthwhile for me.


## Psalms 39:8 (id: 10019039008)

8Please save me from all the ways that I sin against you. Do not let me be someone whom foolish people ridicule.


## Psalms 39:9 (id: 10019039009)

9I kept silent and did not say anything, because {I knew that} you were the one who caused {me to suffer}.


## Psalms 39:10 (id: 10019039010)

10{But now,} please stop punishing me! As for me, because you have afflicted me, I am about to die.


## Psalms 39:11 (id: 10019039011)

11When you correct a person for the wrong things that he has done, you destroy what he treasures, just as moths destroy clothing. Truly, every person lives only a very short time. Selah


## Psalms 39:12 (id: 10019039012)

12Yahweh, please hear me as I pray {to you} and listen {to me} when I cry out for help. Do not ignore my tears, because I am only living before you {on the earth} for a short time, a temporary resident, just like all of my ancestors.


## Psalms 39:13 (id: 10019039013)

13Please leave me alone in order that I may be happy {for awhile} before I die.”


## Psalms 40:0 (id: 10019040000)

0A note for the worship leader: David wrote this psalm.


## Psalms 40:1 (id: 10019040001)

1I patiently waited for Yahweh {to help me,} and he was attentive to me and listened when I called out to him.


## Psalms 40:2 (id: 10019040002)

2When bad things were happening to me, it was as though I was in a dangerous pit that was full of mud and slime but he rescued me from those terrible things as though he had lifted me out of the pit and set me onto a solid rock where I could stand securely.


## Psalms 40:3 (id: 10019040003)

3He gave me reason to sing a new song, praising him, our God. Many people will see {what he did for me} and will revere him and will trust in Yahweh.


## Psalms 40:4 (id: 10019040004)

## Psalms 40:5 (id: 10019040005)

5Yahweh my God, you are the one who has done many wonderful things and made {great} plans for us! No one compares to you. If I tried to tell others about all of those wonderful things, there would be too many for me to mention.


## Psalms 40:6 (id: 10019040006)

6It is not the sacrifices and offerings that you have wanted {from me}; instead, you gave me the ability to hear and obey you. Burnt offerings and sin offerings are not what you require {to forgive my sins}.


## Psalms 40:7 (id: 10019040007)

7So I said, “Yahweh, here I am, {ready to do the things that} the scroll of your instruction says about me.”


## Psalms 40:8 (id: 10019040008)

8My God, I gladly do what you desire; I deeply treasure your instruction.


## Psalms 40:9 (id: 10019040009)

9Whenever your people gather together in the large congregation, I tell them about the righteous things {that you do}. Indeed, Yahweh, certainly you know that I do not stop talking {about this}.


## Psalms 40:10 (id: 10019040010)

10I have not kept to myself {the good news} that you are righteous; I have told the large gathering of your people that you are faithful and that you save us. I have not hidden from them that you faithfully love us and are always trustworthy.


## Psalms 40:11 (id: 10019040011)

11Yahweh, you will not stop being merciful to me. Because you faithfully love me and are always trustworthy, {I know that} you will continually protect me.


## Psalms 40:12 (id: 10019040012)

12I assure myself of this because so many bad things are happening to me that it seems that I cannot even count them. the wrong things that I have done are now affecting me badly, and I cannot see {a way to fix them}. It seems that they are more numerous than the hairs on my head, and I am very discouraged.


## Psalms 40:13 (id: 10019040013)

13Yahweh, please rescue me! Yahweh, please come quickly to help me!


## Psalms 40:14 (id: 10019040014)

14May God completely disgrace all of the people who want to kill me. May God frustrate the people who want me to suffer and may he humiliate them.


## Psalms 40:15 (id: 10019040015)

15May those who mock me, saying, “Ha, Ha!” feel horrified because God has so humiliated them.


## Psalms 40:16 (id: 10019040016)

## Psalms 40:17 (id: 10019040017)

## Psalms 41:8 (id: 10019041008)

8{They say about me,} “Something terrible has happened to him, and he will never get up from where he is lying.”


## Psalms 41:9 (id: 10019041009)

9Even my closest friend, the one I trusted, who shared meals with me, has greatly betrayed me.


## Psalms 41:10 (id: 10019041010)

10However, Yahweh, please be merciful to me and make me well, so that I can do to them what they did to me.


## Psalms 41:11 (id: 10019041011)

11When you do this, I will know that you are pleased with me, when my enemies {will not be able to defeat me and so} cannot rejoice about it.


## Psalms 41:12 (id: 10019041012)

12As for me, you have helped me because I live honestly, and you will allow me to be with you forever.


## Psalms 41:13 (id: 10019041013)

13We should all praise Yahweh, the God whom the people of Israel worship! People should praise him at all times, now and forever! Yes, let it be so!


## Psalms 42:0 (id: 10019042000)

0This is a note for the worship leader: One of the Korahites wrote this song.


## Psalms 42:1 (id: 10019042001)

1When a wild deer is very thirsty, it takes short, quick breaths because it wants so badly to find a brook from which it can drink water. That is how much I want you, God. It is as if I am taking short, quick breaths.


## Psalms 42:2 (id: 10019042002)

2You are the only true God, and I desire very greatly to be in your temple again and worship in your presence. I desire that so much that it is as if I am very thirsty.


## Psalms 42:3 (id: 10019042003)

3I have been crying continually. I have been crying so much that I have not been able to eat. This is because my enemies keep telling me, “Your God will certainly not help you!”


## Psalms 42:4 (id: 10019042004)

4It is appropriate for me to weep to express my great sorrow. But {to encourage myself,} I should also remember how I used to go with large groups of people to celebrate festivals at God’s temple. {Because I was one of the worship musicians,} I would lead the people as they walked to the temple. {On the way there,} we all shouted joyfully and praised God.


## Psalms 42:5 (id: 10019042005)

5So I certainly should not be so sad and upset! I can confidently expect God to rescue me! When he does, I will have the opportunity to praise him as my savior and


## Psalms 42:6 (id: 10019042006)

6my God. But I do feel very sad. So {even though I am a long distance from your temple, God} I will remember you from here at Mount Mizar in the land near Mount Hermon where the Jordan River begins.


## Psalms 42:7 (id: 10019042007)

7The waterfalls you created make such a loud sound that it is as if one deep stream were shouting to another deep stream within the river. I feel such anguish that it is as if one wave after another were crashing over me.


## Psalms 42:8 (id: 10019042008)

8Yahweh continually loves me faithfully as he promised. In response, as he inspires me, I continually sing prayers to him. He is the God who keeps me alive.


## Psalms 42:9 (id: 10019042009)

9God is like a huge rock on top of which I am safe. So I will pray to him, “Please pay attention to me! I am living sorrowfully because my enemies are afflicting me!”


## Psalms 42:10 (id: 10019042010)

10Those enemies are continually insulting me. They keep telling me, “Your God will certainly not help you!” When they insult me like that, it is as painful as if they were breaking my bones.


## Psalms 42:11 (id: 10019042011)

11But I certainly should not be so sad and upset! I can confidently expect God to rescue me! When he does, I will have the opportunity to praise him as my savior and my God.


## Psalms 43:1 (id: 10019043001)

1Please protect me, God, from the people who are being so cruel to me. Please consider that I do not deserve that. Rescue me from people who would deceive me and treat me unfairly.


## Psalms 43:2 (id: 10019043002)

2{I ask you to do this} because you are the God who protects me, and yet I feel as if you have abandoned me! I am living sorrowfully because my enemies are afflicting me.


## Psalms 43:3 (id: 10019043003)

3Treat me favorably, as if you were smiling at me. Help me, as you have promised to do for those who are loyal to you. Guide me so that I can return to your temple on Mount Zion.


## Psalms 43:4 (id: 10019043004)

4{When I do come to Zion,} I will go to your altar {to worship you}. O God, you make me very joyful! {At your altar,} I will sing praise to you, the God whom I worship, as I play the harp.


## Psalms 43:5 (id: 10019043005)

5So I certainly should not be so sad and upset! I can confidently expect God to rescue me! When he does, I will have the opportunity to praise him as my savior and my God.


## Psalms 44:0 (id: 10019044000)

0This is a note for the worship leader: One of the Korahites wrote this song.


## Psalms 44:1 (id: 10019044001)

1God, we listened carefully when our ancestors told us about the great things you did long ago when the Israelites first came into the land of Canaan.


## Psalms 44:2 (id: 10019044002)

2{They told us about} how powerfully you forced the other nations to leave and established our ancestors securely in their land. It was as if they had been plants that you put firmly in the ground. You destroyed the people groups that had lived there and enabled our ancestors to settle there.


## Psalms 44:3 (id: 10019044003)

3Our ancestors did not conquer this land with their own weapons or by their own power. Instead, you helped them and defeated their enemies by your own great power. You did that because you loved them.


## Psalms 44:4 (id: 10019044004)

4You are our real king, O God. Please enable the people of Israel to defeat their enemies.


## Psalms 44:5 (id: 10019044005)

5You enable us to defeat our enemies. Because you help us, we destroy armies that fight against us.


## Psalms 44:6 (id: 10019044006)

6We do not expect to win victories because of the weapons we have.


## Psalms 44:7 (id: 10019044007)

7Instead, you rescue us from our enemies. You disgrace our opponents {by defeating them}.


## Psalms 44:8 (id: 10019044008)

8We regularly celebrate what God has done for us. We will always praise him. Selah


## Psalms 44:9 (id: 10019044009)

9But now it appears that you no longer want to help us. This has embarrassed us. When our armies march out to fight a battle, you no longer enable them to win victories.


## Psalms 44:10 (id: 10019044010)

10You make us retreat from our enemies. We must leave behind weapons and possessions, and they take as many of those as they want.


## Psalms 44:11 (id: 10019044011)

11You have allowed our enemies to slaughter us as if we were sheep. Foreign armies have captured many of our people and taken them back to their countries as slaves.


## Psalms 44:12 (id: 10019044012)

12You did not even receive any money for allowing others to take your people as their slaves. You did not become any richer as a result of that.


## Psalms 44:13 (id: 10019044013)

13Because you no longer help us, the people groups around us insult us. They express contempt for us and laugh at us.


## Psalms 44:14 (id: 10019044014)

14People in other nations are using us Israelites as an example of an unfortunate people group. They are shaking their heads at us to ridicule us.


## Psalms 44:15 (id: 10019044015)

15I am constantly aware of my disgrace. I feel complete shame


## Psalms 44:16 (id: 10019044016)

16because people are speaking so harshly to me. Those people are my enemies, and they want to harm me.


## Psalms 44:17 (id: 10019044017)

17These things have happened to us even though we have not stopped worshiping you and we have not disobeyed the solemn agreement that you made with our ancestors.


## Psalms 44:18 (id: 10019044018)

18We still love you as our God, and we have continued to do what you have commanded us to do.


## Psalms 44:19 (id: 10019044019)

19But {you have allowed} our enemies to ruin our land and leave it desolate. You have left us feeling as though we are going to die.


## Psalms 44:20 (id: 10019044020)

20If we had stopped worshiping you as our God and had started worshiping a foreign god,


## Psalms 44:21 (id: 10019044021)

21you would surely have known that. That is because you know even what people secretly think.


## Psalms 44:22 (id: 10019044022)

22Because you are not helping us, our enemies are continuing to kill our people. They believe they can kill us as easily as if we were sheep, which people slaughter easily.


## Psalms 44:23 (id: 10019044023)

23Protect us! Do not fail to act, Lord, as if you were asleep! Defend us! Do not keep treating us as if we were not your people!


## Psalms 44:24 (id: 10019044024)

24Please be favorable to us and help us! Consider how badly our enemies are harming us and how greatly we are suffering!


## Psalms 44:25 (id: 10019044025)

25We ask you to do this because we are helpless, as if we had fallen down and were lying on the ground and could not get up.


## Psalms 44:26 (id: 10019044026)

26You have promised to love us faithfully because we belong to you. So please do something to rescue us from our enemies!


## Psalms 45:0 (id: 10019045000)

0This is a note for the worship leader: One of the Korahites wrote this song. It is a love song. The tune is “Lilies.”


## Psalms 45:1 (id: 10019045001)

1There is something wonderful that I feel so strongly about that I must sing about it. I will sing a song that I have composed in honor of the king. As I sing, I will express my ideas in an orderly and complete way, just like a scribe who records quickly and accurately what he hears people say.


## Psalms 45:2 (id: 10019045002)

2O King, you are the most handsome man in the world! You speak very graciously! So we know that God has always blessed you.


## Psalms 45:3 (id: 10019045003)

3Prepare yourself to keep fighting powerfully {for justice}! You are glorious and majestic when you do that.


## Psalms 45:4 (id: 10019045004)

4Go and win victories to establish yourself as a king who is honest, who does not proudly oppress his people, and who does what is right. As you do that, may you find that your skill as a warrior terrifies anyone who would resist you.


## Psalms 45:5 (id: 10019045005)

5Your sharp arrows pierce into the hearts of your enemies. You defeat the people groups that fight against you.


## Psalms 45:6 (id: 10019045006)

6Your Majesty, you will be the king for a very long time. You decide people’s cases fairly as you rule your kingdom.


## Psalms 45:7 (id: 10019045007)

7You love it when people do what is right, and you hate it when people do what is wrong. That is why God, whom you worship, has made you so happy. You are happier than all the people around you.


## Psalms 45:8 (id: 10019045008)

8Your royal robes smell beautifully of {the spices} myrrh, aloes, and cassia. From within your palace, which has ivory decorations, you hear people playing stringed instruments. That music makes you happy.


## Psalms 45:9 (id: 10019045009)

9The honored women of your court include foreign princesses. {Your bride,} the queen, is standing at your right side. Threads of gold fine gold from Ophir decorate her robe.


## Psalms 45:10 (id: 10019045010)

10And as for you, dear bride, please listen carefully to my advice. From now on, regard this as your country and these people as your family.


## Psalms 45:11 (id: 10019045011)

11If you do that, the king will love and appreciate you even more. Bow down in front of him to honor him as your husband and king.


## Psalms 45:12 (id: 10019045012)

12Representatives of {foreign kingdoms such as the wealthy kingdom} of Tyre will bring gifts to you. The wealthy people of this kingdom will ask you to treat them favorably.


## Psalms 45:13 (id: 10019045013)

13This royal princess looked so beautiful in her private room where she had dressed for the wedding! She had put on a robe that gold threads richly decorated.


## Psalms 45:14 (id: 10019045014)

14As she came in procession to where you are, O king, people could see that her robe also had beautiful designs of colored thread. Other young women, her bridesmaids, followed her in that procession.


## Psalms 45:15 (id: 10019045015)

15As they came in procession into your palace, this made everyone very happy.


## Psalms 45:16 (id: 10019045016)

16You will be famous not because of your ancestors but because of your descendants. They will have influence and authority all over the world.


## Psalms 45:17 (id: 10019045017)

17{By this song,} I want to make the people of future generations remember what a great king you are. Then people will always say good things about you.


## Psalms 46:0 (id: 10019046000)

0This is a note for the worship leader: One of the Korahites wrote this song. The tune is “Maidens.”


## Psalms 46:1 (id: 10019046001)

1God keeps us safe, as if he were a secure place where we could go if we were in danger. God strengthens us. He is always present to help us when we have troubles.


## Psalms 46:2 (id: 10019046002)

2So we would not be afraid even if there were an earthquake that caused huge chunks of mountains to fall deep into the sea.


## Psalms 46:3 (id: 10019046003)

3We would not be afraid even if there were a great storm in the ocean whose surging waves battered the coastline. Selah


## Psalms 46:4 (id: 10019046004)

4A stream flows down from the mountains into Jerusalem. Its cool waters refresh the people who live there. Jerusalem is the location of God’s holy temple. He is greater than all other spirit beings.


## Psalms 46:5 (id: 10019046005)

5God is present in Jerusalem in a special way, so no one destroys it. God rescues the people of that city, and it is as if a dark night ends and a bright new day begins.


## Psalms 46:6 (id: 10019046006)

6Sometimes foreign armies invade kingdoms and conquer them. {But that does not happen to us, because} God powerfully defends us and defeats our enemies.


## Psalms 46:7 (id: 10019046007)

7Yahweh, the commander of the heavenly armies, helps us. The God whom we Israelites worship keeps us safe. Selah


## Psalms 46:8 (id: 10019046008)

8Let me tell you what Yahweh does. He destroys our enemies.


## Psalms 46:9 (id: 10019046009)

9He stops wars all over the world. It is as if he breaks bows and arrows, destroys spears, and burns up war wagons.


## Psalms 46:10 (id: 10019046010)

10God says, “Calm down and remember that I am God. The people of nations throughout the earth will honor me.”


## Psalms 46:11 (id: 10019046011)

11Yahweh, the commander of the heavenly armies, helps us. The God whom we Israelites worship keeps us safe. Selah


## Psalms 47:0 (id: 10019047000)

0This is a note for the worship leader: One of the Korahites wrote this psalm.


## Psalms 47:1 (id: 10019047001)

1People all over the world, clap your hands! Shout joyfully to praise God!


## Psalms 47:2 (id: 10019047002)

2Everyone should revere Yahweh, who is much greater than any other spirit being. He rules powerfully over the whole world!


## Psalms 47:3 (id: 10019047003)

3He enables us to conquer people groups that oppose us.


## Psalms 47:4 (id: 10019047004)

4He loved our ancestor Jacob, and so he chose this land to be our possession. We are proud of the land he gave us. Selah


## Psalms 47:5 (id: 10019047005)

5People have acclaimed Yahweh their God as their king by shouting and blowing ram’s horns.


## Psalms 47:6 (id: 10019047006)

6Sing songs to praise God our King! Yes, sing songs to praise him!


## Psalms 47:7 (id: 10019047007)

7Do that because God rules the whole world. Sing a special song about that.


## Psalms 47:8 (id: 10019047008)

8God rules over all people groups. He rules them completely fairly, not the way many human kings do.


## Psalms 47:9 (id: 10019047009)

9The rulers of the people groups will assemble to join those who worship the same God whom Abraham worshiped. They will do that because the leaders of the world must obey God. He is the supreme ruler of the world.


## Psalms 48:0 (id: 10019048000)

0One of the Korahites wrote this psalm.


## Psalms 48:1 (id: 10019048001)

1Yahweh is great, and he deserves to have people praise him greatly in {Jerusalem}, the city that belongs to him. That city is high up on the mountain that he chose as the place where people would worship him.


## Psalms 48:2 (id: 10019048002)

2The height of Mount Zion is magnificent. People from all over the world are happy when they see it. The city {of Jerusalem,} where Yahweh rules as the great king, is on the northern ridge of that mountain.


## Psalms 48:3 (id: 10019048003)

3God is present {as a defender} in the city’s fortifications. He has shown that he protects the people of the city.


## Psalms 48:4 (id: 10019048004)

4Listen to why Yahweh deserves our praise. Kings joined together and led their armies to attack our city.


## Psalms 48:5 (id: 10019048005)

5However, when they saw it, its secure position and defenses amazed them. They became so frightened that they ran away.


## Psalms 48:6 (id: 10019048006)

6Their bodies shook because they were so afraid. They screamed as if they were women who were giving birth.


## Psalms 48:7 (id: 10019048007)

7Just as you, Yahweh, can sent a strong east wind that wrecks even the sturdiest ships at sea{, so you destroyed the armies that were attacking us}.


## Psalms 48:8 (id: 10019048008)

8Jerusalem belongs to Yahweh, the commander of the heavenly armies, who is our God. We had heard {from our ancestors} that God would always keep the city secure. Now we who live in the city have seen for ourselves that this is true. Selah


## Psalms 48:9 (id: 10019048009)

9We went into your temple, God, and recognized that you faithfully kept your covenant when you protected us. We acknowledged that you had been just as faithful to us as you were to our ancestors.


## Psalms 48:10 (id: 10019048010)

10People throughout the world praise you as you deserve. You use your power to make sure that people treat each other right.


## Psalms 48:11 (id: 10019048011)

11The people who live in Jerusalem and in other parts of the kingdom of Judah should celebrate the way you judge people fairly.


## Psalms 48:12 (id: 10019048012)

12You Judeans should walk around the walls of the city of Jerusalem. See how many defensive towers it has!


## Psalms 48:13 (id: 10019048013)

13You should observe the strength of the walls and the fortifications so that you can tell your descendants about them.


## Psalms 48:14 (id: 10019048014)

14The people of Judah and Jerusalem should praise Yahweh because he is our God and he will always be our God. He will lead us for as long as we live.


## Psalms 49:0 (id: 10019049000)

0This is a note for the worship leader: One of the Korahites wrote this psalm.


## Psalms 49:1 (id: 10019049001)

1Listen to me, members of people groups from throughout the world,


## Psalms 49:2 (id: 10019049002)

2whether or not you are famous and whether or not you are rich!


## Psalms 49:3 (id: 10019049003)

3I have thought carefully about what I am going to say, so it will be wise, and it will help you understand things better.


## Psalms 49:4 (id: 10019049004)

4There is a popular saying about something that people find difficult to understand. I have considered it, and I will explain it as I {sing and} play my harp.


## Psalms 49:5 (id: 10019049005)

5I do not have to be afraid during difficult times when wicked people around me try to harm me.


## Psalms 49:6 (id: 10019049006)

6These wicked people believe that they will be able to use their money to overcome any difficulty. They boast about how rich they are.


## Psalms 49:7 (id: 10019049007)

7No one can use his money to keep a person from dying. No, no one can pay God money so that another person will not die.


## Psalms 49:8 (id: 10019049008)

8No matter how much money a person has, it is never enough to keep someone else from dying


## Psalms 49:9 (id: 10019049009)

9so that the person will live forever and never leave this world.


## Psalms 49:10 (id: 10019049010)

10Whether people are wise or foolish and brutish, they all die. Then other people inherit their money.


## Psalms 49:11 (id: 10019049011)

11They imagine that the houses they live in will always exist. They call their properties by their own names {as if they would always have them}.


## Psalms 49:12 (id: 10019049012)

12Even if someone is great, he does not stay that way. He still dies, just as animals do.


## Psalms 49:13 (id: 10019049013)

13Those who think and act as if they will never die are living in a foolish manner. Even so, some other people admire them and approve of what they say. Selah


## Psalms 49:14 (id: 10019049014)

14God has determined that they will die, as easily as sheep die, and go to the place where dead people are. They will remain there, as if death were a shepherd keeping them in a pen. One day God will make righteous people rule over wicked people. Wicked people will no longer live in comfortable homes. Instead, their bodies will decay in their graves.


## Psalms 49:15 (id: 10019049015)

15But God will take me to himself. In that way, he will rescue me from the place where dead people are. Selah


## Psalms 49:16 (id: 10019049016)

16Do not let it trouble you when a person becomes rich and has many valuable possessions.


## Psalms 49:17 (id: 10019049017)

17After all, when that person dies, he will take nothing with him {to the place where dead people are}. He will not be able to bring his money there with him.


## Psalms 49:18 (id: 10019049018)

18While a rich person is alive, he says to himself that he is successful. (People tend to praise anyone who is successful.)


## Psalms 49:19 (id: 10019049019)

19Nevertheless, he will die and go to where his dead ancestors are. All of them will remain in that dark place forever.


## Psalms 49:20 (id: 10019049020)

20If someone is great but does not understand these things, he will die, just as animals do.


## Psalms 50:1 (id: 10019050001)

1A Levite from the clan of Asaph wrote this song. Yahweh is the only true God. He has decreed that people from all over the world should come to him.


## Psalms 50:2 (id: 10019050002)

2Mount Zion is as wonderful as a place can be {because God’s temple is there}. God’s presence there is like a light that shines brightly.


## Psalms 50:3 (id: 10019050003)

3Our God is approaching us {to judge us}. He will now tell us {what we have done right and what we have done wrong}. He is coming very powerfully, as he did at Mount Sinai, when there was a great thunderstorm and a burning fire on the mountain as he approached it.


## Psalms 50:4 (id: 10019050004)

4He calls upon the sky and the land {to serve as witnesses of his covenant with the Israelites}. This demonstrates that he will judge them fairly {according to that covenant}.


## Psalms 50:5 (id: 10019050005)

5He says, “Summon those who worship me faithfully, those who made a covenant with me that involved offering sacrifices to me.”


## Psalms 50:6 (id: 10019050006)

6The sky displays the character of God so clearly, it is as though it is saying, “God is righteous, and he is the supreme judge.” Selah


## Psalms 50:7 (id: 10019050007)

7God says, “You Israelite people who belong to me, listen as I speak to you. I am the God whom you worship. I am going to tell you what you have done right and what you have done wrong.


## Psalms 50:8 (id: 10019050008)

8You continually burn animals completely on the temple altar as sacrifices to me, so I have no complaint that you have failed to offer sacrifices to me.


## Psalms 50:9 (id: 10019050009)

9However, I do not really need the bulls from your herds or the goats from your pens {that you sacrifice to me}.


## Psalms 50:10 (id: 10019050010)

10I do not need them, because all of the animals that live in the forests belong to me. All of the cattle everywhere on earth also belong to me.


## Psalms 50:11 (id: 10019050011)

11I own all the birds that live in the mountains. All the little creatures that move around in the fields also belong to me.


## Psalms 50:12 (id: 10019050012)

12Because everything in the world belongs to me, if I were hungry, I would not tell you {so that you would bring me some food to eat}.


## Psalms 50:13 (id: 10019050013)

13When you sacrifice bulls to me, I do not eat their flesh, and when you offer goats to me, I do not drink their blood.


## Psalms 50:14 (id: 10019050014)

14I want you to offer sacrifices to me, your God, because you have promised to do that to thank me for helping you. I am the God who is greater than all other spirit beings.


## Psalms 50:15 (id: 10019050015)

15So pray to me when you are in trouble. If you do that, I will rescue you, and then you will honor me {publicly by offering a sacrifice}.”


## Psalms 50:16 (id: 10019050016)

16But God says to wicked people, “You should not be reciting my commandments or talking about the covenant that I made with you!


## Psalms 50:17 (id: 10019050017)

17You should not do that, because you hate it when I correct you, and when I tell you to do something, you refuse to obey me.


## Psalms 50:18 (id: 10019050018)

18When you see someone stealing, you decide that it is acceptable for him to steal. When you learn that people are having sexual relations with people other than their spouses, you do the same thing.


## Psalms 50:19 (id: 10019050019)

19You do not restrain yourselves from saying wicked things, and you make up things to say to deceive people.


## Psalms 50:20 (id: 10019050020)

20You consistently say bad things about members of your own family, even about your closest relatives.


## Psalms 50:21 (id: 10019050021)

21You do all those things, and I have not said anything to you about them, so you think that I do not care about them any more than you do. But now I am going to show you {all the wicked things you have done}, and I am going to judge and punish you for doing them.


## Psalms 50:22 (id: 10019050022)

22Many of you have been ignoring me. Pay attention to what I am saying now! If you do not, I will destroy you. No one will be able to rescue you.


## Psalms 50:23 (id: 10019050023)

23When people publicly offer sacrifices to thank me for helping them, that honors me greatly. If people live as I want people to live, I will rescue them when they are in trouble.”


## Psalms 51:0 (id: 10019051000)

0This is a note for the worship leader: King David wrote this song after the prophet Nathan came and rebuked him for having sexual relations with {another man’s wife,} Bathsheba.


## Psalms 51:1 (id: 10019051001)

1Oh God, since you promised to faithfully love me, please act mercifully toward me. You gently love people in many ways, so please forgive me for disobeying you so badly.


## Psalms 51:2 (id: 10019051002)

2Remove my sin completely, as if it were dirt that you were washing off me to make me clean.


## Psalms 51:3 (id: 10019051003)

3I ask this because I know that I have disobeyed you in many ways. I am continually aware of that.


## Psalms 51:4 (id: 10019051004)

4What grieves me most is how I have sinned against you. I have done things that you consider to be very wrong. So when you declare that I am guilty, you are right. When you judge me, you are completely fair.


## Psalms 51:5 (id: 10019051005)

5Indeed, I have been a sinner from birth. In fact, I have been a sinner since my mother conceived me.


## Psalms 51:6 (id: 10019051006)

6I know that you want me to have honest intentions. You help me understand very well how to act wisely.


## Psalms 51:7 (id: 10019051007)

7Make me acceptable to you again, as if you were using hyssop to sprinkle water on me to purify me. Remove my sin as if it were dirt that you were washing off me. Then I will be completely pure, like snow that has just fallen.


## Psalms 51:8 (id: 10019051008)

8Please tell me {that you have forgiven me}. That would make me very happy. My guilt feels like a heavy weight on me, but may I rejoice {in your forgiveness}.


## Psalms 51:9 (id: 10019051009)

9Please forgive all the wrong things I have done so that it will be as if I never did them.


## Psalms 51:10 (id: 10019051010)

10O God, please make me want to do only things that please you. Help me to be loyal to you consistently.


## Psalms 51:11 (id: 10019051011)

11Please allow me to come into your presence. Please do not make your Holy Spirit leave me.


## Psalms 51:12 (id: 10019051012)

12Please save me {from the guilt and punishment of my sin}. That will make me very happy again. Please make me eager to obey you so that I do not sin again.


## Psalms 51:13 (id: 10019051013)

13Please make me so obedient that I can even teach people who are doing wrong things how you want them to live instead. That way people who have been disobeying you will start obeying you again.


## Psalms 51:14 (id: 10019051014)

14God, I admit that I deserve to die because I murdered someone, but please rescue me from that punishment. You can save me {from the guilt and punishment of my sin} because you are God. When you do that, I will sing joyfully about how good you are.


## Psalms 51:15 (id: 10019051015)

15Lord, {please do these things so that} I will have a reason to speak publicly and say things to praise you.


## Psalms 51:16 (id: 10019051016)

16If you had wanted me to offer a sacrifice {because of the sin I committed}, I would certainly have done that. But you do not accept burnt offerings {from people who bring them to try to make things right with you after committing murder}.


## Psalms 51:17 (id: 10019051017)

17What you really want is for people to pray to you in genuine humility {and ask for forgiveness}. When a person is very sorrowful for his sin, you, God, do not reject him.


## Psalms 51:18 (id: 10019051018)

18Please regard us Israelites favorably and help us. Protect us from our enemies.


## Psalms 51:19 (id: 10019051019)

19Then people will offer sacrifices to you for the right reasons. They will bring animals that they will burn completely as offerings to you. They will completely burn up young bulls on your altar. You will gladly accept these sacrifices.


## Psalms 52:0 (id: 10019052000)

0This is a note for the worship leader: David wrote this song when Doeg the Edomite went to King Saul and told him, “David went to Ahimelech’s house.”


## Psalms 52:1 (id: 10019052001)

1{Doeg,} you are boasting as if you were a great warrior because you killed so many people{, but you are wrong to boast, because they were helpless}. God always protects me because he is kind to me.


## Psalms 52:2 (id: 10019052002)

2You say things that you know will cause great harm to other people. It is as if your tongue is a sharp knife slashing people. People did something innocently, but you reported it in such a way that they seemed to be guilty.


## Psalms 52:3 (id: 10019052003)

3You like to do what is wrong more than you like to do what is right. You like to tell lies more than you like to tell the truth. Selah


## Psalms 52:4 (id: 10019052004)

4You like to say anything that will cause harm to people. What you say causes innocent people to suffer.


## Psalms 52:5 (id: 10019052005)

5But God will destroy you permanently, just as you have destroyed others. You may feel safe in your home, but God will take you from there and remove you from this world in which people are alive. Selah


## Psalms 52:6 (id: 10019052006)

6When good people see that, they will respect God {for being just}. They will laugh at you.


## Psalms 52:7 (id: 10019052007)

7{They will say,} “Look what happened to this man who did not trust God to protect him. He thought that because he had a lot of money, he could avoid trouble. He believed that harming other people would make him powerful.”


## Psalms 52:8 (id: 10019052008)

8But I am safe, just like the olive trees that the priests plant and care for in the courtyard of the place where we worship God. I know that God will always be kind to me.


## Psalms 52:9 (id: 10019052009)

9I am confident that you, God, are going to do what I have described, and I will always praise you for that. You have demonstrated to the people who are faithful to you that you are good. So I will wait patiently for you to act according to your good character.


## Psalms 53:0 (id: 10019053000)

0This is a note for the worship leader: David wrote this song to the tune of “Weakness.”


## Psalms 53:1 (id: 10019053001)

1People who do not want to obey God tell themselves that God does not exist. People like that are bad. They do terrible things. Every one of them acts wickedly.


## Psalms 53:2 (id: 10019053002)

2God looks down from heaven at the people on the earth. He wants to find out whether any of them are wise and want to know him.


## Psalms 53:3 (id: 10019053003)

3But they have all chosen to disobey God. They have all become bad people. Every single one of them acts wickedly.


## Psalms 53:4 (id: 10019053004)

4They take away people’s possessions until they have nothing left. They do that as easily as they would eat a loaf of bread. The people who do such bad things ought to realize {that God is going to punish them}. But they do not worship God.


## Psalms 53:5 (id: 10019053005)

5An army came and attacked the Israelites. Its soldiers did not have to be afraid that the Israelites would defeat them. But God made them lose in battle so badly that the survivors were not even able to bury the dead soldiers. This made them very afraid. God regarded this army as insignificant {compared with his great power}. {He helped} the Israelites{, and so they} were able to humiliate those soldiers {by defeating them}.


## Psalms 53:6 (id: 10019053006)

6I wish that God, whose sanctuary is on Mount Zion, would rescue the Israelite people! When God makes his people prosperous again, the Israelites, those descendants of Jacob, will be very happy.


## Psalms 54:0 (id: 10019054000)

0This is a note for the worship leader: Musicians playing stringed instruments should accompany this song. David wrote this song when the people of the region of Ziph went to Saul and told him that David was hiding in their area.


## Psalms 54:1 (id: 10019054001)

1God, please use your power to save me {from my enemies}! If you do that, people will realize that I have not done anything wrong.


## Psalms 54:2 (id: 10019054002)

2God, please listen to what I say as I pray to you.


## Psalms 54:3 (id: 10019054003)

3I am praying for your help because even my fellow Judeans are treacherously acting against me. These are violent men, and they want to kill me. They do not respect you. Selah


## Psalms 54:4 (id: 10019054004)

4But God is helping me. The Lord is helping the people who are protecting me so that I stay alive.


## Psalms 54:5 (id: 10019054005)

5My enemies want to do harmful things to me, but God will cause those things to happen to them instead. Because you faithfully do what you promise, destroy them {so that they do not kill me}!


## Psalms 54:6 (id: 10019054006)

6I promise that {when you rescue me,} I will publicly offer a sacrifice to honor you. I will praise you, Yahweh, for how good you are.


## Psalms 54:7 (id: 10019054007)

7I will be able to do this because he rescues me from all my troubles and, as a result, I see that he has defeated my enemies.


## Psalms 55:0 (id: 10019055000)

0This is a note for the worship leader: Musicians playing stringed instruments should accompany this song. David wrote this song.


## Psalms 55:1 (id: 10019055001)

1God, please listen to me as I pray to you. Please do not ignore me as I am pleading with you.


## Psalms 55:2 (id: 10019055002)

2Please pay attention to me and do what I ask. The things I am praying about are agitating me and making me moan {in distress}.


## Psalms 55:3 (id: 10019055003)

3This is because my enemies are saying things {to threaten me}. Wicked people are attacking me. They are causing me to have great troubles. Because they are so hostile to me, they never stop trying to harm me.


## Psalms 55:4 (id: 10019055004)

4My heart is pounding inside me because I am so upset. I strongly feel many fears because I know that my enemies are trying to kill me.


## Psalms 55:5 (id: 10019055005)

5I am shaking because I am so afraid. The only thing I feel is fear.


## Psalms 55:6 (id: 10019055006)

6I have been thinking, “How good it would be if I had wings like a dove! I would fly away and find a place to rest.


## Psalms 55:7 (id: 10019055007)

7If I could, I would go far away and stay in a desolate area. Selah


## Psalms 55:8 (id: 10019055008)

8I would quickly find a safe place where my enemies could not harm me in the way that a powerful storm causes damage.”


## Psalms 55:9 (id: 10019055009)

9Lord, please destroy my enemies! Make them disagree about what to do {so that they cannot make successful plans against me}. I ask this because I know that they are fighting with people in the city and hurting them.


## Psalms 55:10 (id: 10019055010)

10The fighting is constant, and the people in the city cannot get away from it. Bad people are causing much trouble right in the middle of the city.


## Psalms 55:11 (id: 10019055011)

11These bad people harm others right in the middle of the city. Dishonest people continually deceive and cheat others in the marketplace.


## Psalms 55:12 (id: 10019055012)

12If it were an enemy who was saying bad things about me, I could endure it. If it were someone who hated me who was powerfully opposing me, I could hide from him.


## Psalms 55:13 (id: 10019055013)

13But the person who is doing those things to me is someone I treated not as a subject but as an equal. I knew him well, and we were together often.


## Psalms 55:14 (id: 10019055014)

14We used to talk together in a wonderful way. We also used to go to the temple courtyard together {to worship God} with the other Israelites who gathered there.


## Psalms 55:15 (id: 10019055015)

15My enemies are so wicked in their inner beings that it is as if wickedness lives in their homes {and influences what they do}. So I hope that bad things happen to them that destroy them! I wish that {the earth would open up under them so that} they would go down alive to the place where dead people are.


## Psalms 55:16 (id: 10019055016)

16But I will ask Yahweh, my God, to help me, and he will rescue me.


## Psalms 55:17 (id: 10019055017)

17I will continually pray to him, whether I can express my concerns in words or whether I am only able to moan {as a plea to him}. He will answer my prayer by helping me.


## Psalms 55:18 (id: 10019055018)

18My enemies are coming with many soldiers to fight against me, but God will keep me safe and save my life when we fight a battle.


## Psalms 55:19 (id: 10019055019)

19My enemies have no difficulties in life, so they do not respect God. Selah But God, who has ruled forever, will answer my prayer and punish them as they deserve.


## Psalms 55:20 (id: 10019055020)

20The man I knew well is attacking me even though I was his friend. He should have remained loyal to me, but he has not.


## Psalms 55:21 (id: 10019055021)

21What he said was as easy to believe as butter is easy to swallow, but secretly he hated me. The words he spoke were as soothing as olive oil, but all along he intended to use words to hurt people as sharp swords do.


## Psalms 55:22 (id: 10019055022)
