1Sing joyfully to God, who makes us strong! Shout with joy to the God whom we Israelites worship!


## Psalms 81:2 (id: 10019081002)

2Start playing music! Beat tambourines and play lyres and pleasant\-sounding harps.


## Psalms 81:3 (id: 10019081003)

3Blow a ram’s horn for the new\-moon festival {each month} and on the day of the full moon when the Festival of Shelters begins.


## Psalms 81:4 (id: 10019081004)

4Do this because the God whom we Israelite people worship has commanded us to do it.


## Psalms 81:5 (id: 10019081005)

5God told our ancestors to observe these festivals when he delivered them from slavery to the Egyptians, a people whose language they did not understand.


## Psalms 81:6 (id: 10019081006)

6{Yahweh says,} “They had to hold on to heavy baskets full of bricks as they carried them on their shoulders, but I freed them from doing that.


## Psalms 81:7 (id: 10019081007)

7When they were suffering, they cried to me for help, and I rescued them. {At Mount Sinai,} I spoke to them from dark clouds {and gave them laws}. Then I saw whether they trusted me when they complained at Meribah that they had no water. Selah


## Psalms 81:8 (id: 10019081008)

8Listen to me, my people, while I warn you! I wish that you Israelites would pay attention to me!


## Psalms 81:9 (id: 10019081009)

9You must not make any idols that represent any of the gods that other nations worship. You must never bow down to worship any of those gods.


## Psalms 81:10 (id: 10019081010)

10I am Yahweh your God. I am the one who rescued you from slavery in Egypt. If you depend on me, I will provide everything that you need.


## Psalms 81:11 (id: 10019081011)

11However, my people would not listen to what I said; the Israelites refused to obey me.


## Psalms 81:12 (id: 10019081012)

12So I let them do whatever they stubbornly wanted to do; they did whatever they had decided to do.


## Psalms 81:13 (id: 10019081013)

13I wish that my people would obey me! Yes, I wish that the Israelites would live the way I want them to!


## Psalms 81:14 (id: 10019081014)

14Then I would use my power against those who are opposing them, and I would easily defeat their enemies.


## Psalms 81:15 (id: 10019081015)

15Then those who had rebelled against Yahweh would bow down fearfully in front of him {to submit to his authority}. But the Israelites would always prosper.


## Psalms 81:16 (id: 10019081016)

16I would make their fields produce abundant crops, and they would even find all the honey they wanted in wild bee hives in rocky places.”


## Psalms 82:1 (id: 10019082001)

1A Levite from the clan of Asaph wrote this song. God has taken his place in the assembly that he governs; he pronounces judgment on the rulers there.


## Psalms 82:2 (id: 10019082002)

2He says, “You must stop making unjust decisions and favoring wicked people! Selah


## Psalms 82:3 (id: 10019082003)

3Defend poor people and orphans, and decide cases fairly for people who have no one else to help them.


## Psalms 82:4 (id: 10019082004)

4Save defenseless people from what wicked people want to do to them!


## Psalms 82:5 (id: 10019082005)

5You are foolish and ignorant! You do not recognize the right things to do. People are no longer living by the basic principles that make a society fair.


## Psalms 82:6 (id: 10019082006)

6I said, ‘You are spiritual beings; you are all rulers over creation like the Most High.


## Psalms 82:7 (id: 10019082007)

7But you will die as humans do; you will lose your power just as rulers do.’”


## Psalms 82:8 (id: 10019082008)

8God, you rule all the nations, so take action and judge the people who live on the earth!


## Psalms 83:0 (id: 10019083000)

0A Levite from the clan of Asaph wrote this song.


## Psalms 83:1 (id: 10019083001)

1God, please take action to help us! Please do that right away!


## Psalms 83:2 (id: 10019083002)

2I ask this because the enemies who hate you are defiantly approaching us with a large army.


## Psalms 83:3 (id: 10019083003)

3They have plotted deviously together to fight against us Israelites whom you cherish.


## Psalms 83:4 (id: 10019083004)

4They have said to each other, “Come on, let us destroy them so completely that no one will ever remember that Israel existed!”


## Psalms 83:5 (id: 10019083005)

5They have all agreed to do this, and they have made an alliance against you.


## Psalms 83:6 (id: 10019083006)

6This army includes soldiers who are Edomites, Ishmaelites, Moabites, Hagrites,


## Psalms 83:7 (id: 10019083007)

7Gebalites, Ammonites, Amalekites, Philistines, and Tyrians.


## Psalms 83:8 (id: 10019083008)

8The Assyrians are helping them. They have given military support to the Moabites and Ammonites {who are leading this army}. Selah


## Psalms 83:9 (id: 10019083009)

9Defeat them just as you helped Gideon defeat the Midianites, and just as you helped Barak defeat Sisera{, the commander of the Canaanite king} Jabin at the Kishon River!


## Psalms 83:10 (id: 10019083010)

10You destroyed those Canaanites at En Dor, and their dead bodies rotted on the ground.


## Psalms 83:11 (id: 10019083011)

11Kill the leaders of this invading army just as Gideon killed Oreb and Zeeb, the Midianite commanders. Defeat all their rulers just as Gideon defeated Zebah and Zalmunna{, the Midianite rulers}.


## Psalms 83:12 (id: 10019083012)

12They too had said, “Let us take for ourselves the land where God’s people dwell.”


## Psalms 83:13 (id: 10019083013)

13My God, please defeat them so that they run away just as tumbleweed rolls away and just as chaff flies away in the wind!


## Psalms 83:14 (id: 10019083014)

14Just as a forest fire burns down trees and flames burn up the dry vegetation on hillsides,


## Psalms 83:15 (id: 10019083015)

15make them run away afraid as if a great storm were driving them off.


## Psalms 83:16 (id: 10019083016)

16Completely disgrace them so that they will acknowledge who you are, Yahweh.


## Psalms 83:17 (id: 10019083017)

17Embarrass them and make them always afraid. Humiliate them until they die.


## Psalms 83:18 (id: 10019083018)

18Then they will know that you alone, Yahweh, are the Most High God who rules over everyone in the world.


## Psalms 84:0 (id: 10019084000)

0This is a note for the worship leader: Accompany the song with a stringed instrument. One of the Korahites wrote this psalm.


## Psalms 84:1 (id: 10019084001)

1Yahweh, commander of the heavenly armies, what a wonderful place your temple is!


## Psalms 84:2 (id: 10019084002)

2I long greatly, deep inside, to be in the courtyards of Yahweh’s temple. With my whole being I call out to the only true God.


## Psalms 84:3 (id: 10019084003)

3Even little birds have built their nests near your temple, and that is where they are taking care of their babies. Yahweh, commander of the heavenly armies, you are my king and my God.


## Psalms 84:4 (id: 10019084004)

4What a good life people have who live by your temple! They worship you continually there. Selah


## Psalms 84:5 (id: 10019084005)

5What a good life people have whom you make strong so that they can travel to your temple as they greatly desire!


## Psalms 84:6 (id: 10019084006)

6As they pass through the {arid} Valley of Baca, they dig wells to look for water, and God sends autumn rains that fill those wells with life\-giving water.


## Psalms 84:7 (id: 10019084007)

7They become stronger as they go, and each of them arrives at the temple in Zion where God is present.


## Psalms 84:8 (id: 10019084008)

8Yahweh, you are the God who commands the heavenly armies. Please answer my prayer. You are the God whom we Israelite people worship. Please do what I ask. Selah


## Psalms 84:9 (id: 10019084009)

9God, please treat favorably the king you have appointed to protect us.


## Psalms 84:10 (id: 10019084010)

10It is better be in your temple courtyards for a single day than to be anywhere else for a thousand days. I would rather stand at the entrance to the courtyard of my God’s temple than stay in any of the {comfortable} homes where wicked people live.


## Psalms 84:11 (id: 10019084011)

11Yahweh our God makes us prosper, just as the sun makes crops grow, and he protects us, just as a shield protects a warrior. He is kind to us and honors us. Yahweh gives people who live rightly every good thing that they need.


## Psalms 84:12 (id: 10019084012)

12Yahweh, commander of the heavenly armies, what a good life people have who trust you!


## Psalms 85:0 (id: 10019085000)

0This is a note for the worship leader: One of the Korahites wrote this psalm.


## Psalms 85:1 (id: 10019085001)

1Yahweh, you have brought back the Israelites who were exiles so that this land can be prosperous again.


## Psalms 85:2 (id: 10019085002)

2You forgave all the sins that your people committed. Selah


## Psalms 85:3 (id: 10019085003)

3You completely ceased being angry with us and stopped punishing us severely.


## Psalms 85:4 (id: 10019085004)

4God, you are the one who saves us. Please stop being upset with us, and make us strong again.


## Psalms 85:5 (id: 10019085005)

5Please do not be continually angry with us and with our children and grandchildren.


## Psalms 85:6 (id: 10019085006)

6Please help us so that we will thrive again. Then we, your people, will be happy because of you.


## Psalms 85:7 (id: 10019085007)

7Yahweh, please treat us kindly as you promised and rescue us from our troubles.


## Psalms 85:8 (id: 10019085008)

8I am going to listen to what Yahweh, my God, says to me. I believe he will say that he will make his people thrive if they are loyal to him. However, they must not do foolish things anymore.


## Psalms 85:9 (id: 10019085009)

9He is surely ready to help people who deeply respect him. Then he will be gloriously present with us in our land.


## Psalms 85:10 (id: 10019085010)

10He will be faithfully kind and true to us. He will do what is right and make us peaceful.


## Psalms 85:11 (id: 10019085011)

11People on earth will be faithful to God, and God will act justly toward us from heaven.


## Psalms 85:12 (id: 10019085012)

12Yes, Yahweh will give us good things, and our land will produce abundant harvests.


## Psalms 85:13 (id: 10019085013)

13As he considers what to do, he will recognize what the right thing is to do, and he will do that.


## Psalms 86:1 (id: 10019086001)

1A prayer of David. Yahweh, I am poor and helpless, so please do what I ask.


## Psalms 86:2 (id: 10019086002)

2I am loyal to you, so please protect me. I worship you as my God and I trust you, so please save me.


## Psalms 86:3 (id: 10019086003)

3I have been crying out to you continually, Lord, so please be kind to me.


## Psalms 86:4 (id: 10019086004)

4I am asking you to help me, Lord, as someone who worships you, so please make me happy by doing that.


## Psalms 86:5 (id: 10019086005)

5Do this, Lord, because you are good and you forgive people’s sins. You very faithfully love everyone who prays to you.


## Psalms 86:6 (id: 10019086006)

6Yahweh, I am pleading with you in prayer to help me; please do what I ask.


## Psalms 86:7 (id: 10019086007)

7Whenever I am in trouble, I call out to you, because I know that you will help me.


## Psalms 86:8 (id: 10019086008)

8Lord, among all the gods that the nations worship, there is none like you; none of them has done the great things that you have done.


## Psalms 86:9 (id: 10019086009)

9Lord, the nations that you have made will all come and bow down before you, and they will praise you.


## Psalms 86:10 (id: 10019086010)

10They will do this because you are great and you do amazing things; you are the only true God.


## Psalms 86:11 (id: 10019086011)

11Yahweh, please teach me how you want me to live so that I will act properly, as you wish. Give me a sincere desire to honor you.


## Psalms 86:12 (id: 10019086012)

12Lord my God, I will thank you wholeheartedly, and I will praise you forever.


## Psalms 86:13 (id: 10019086013)

13I will do that because you have been so faithfully kind to me; you have kept me from going way down into the place where dead people are.


## Psalms 86:14 (id: 10019086014)

14God, proud people are attacking me. Yes, a group of cruel people is trying to kill me; those people do not respect you.


## Psalms 86:15 (id: 10019086015)

15But you, Lord, are a God who is merciful and kind. You do not quickly become angry, and you are so faithfully kind and true to us.


## Psalms 86:16 (id: 10019086016)

16Consider my situation and be kind to me; make me, your servant, strong. Help me as if I had been born in your household.


## Psalms 86:17 (id: 10019086017)

17Do something to show that you are helping me so that my enemies will see it, and it will embarrass them. I believe that you, Yahweh, will help me and comfort me.


## Psalms 87:1 (id: 10019087001)

1One of the Korahites wrote this song. Yahweh established his city on mountains that he made holy.


## Psalms 87:2 (id: 10019087002)

2He loves the city of Zion more than any other place where Israelites live.


## Psalms 87:3 (id: 10019087003)

3People say wonderful things about this city that belongs to God. Selah


## Psalms 87:4 (id: 10019087004)

4{Yahweh says,} “When I talk about people who know me, this will include Egyptians and Babylonians. Indeed, I will also say about Philistines and Tyrians and Cushites, ‘This person is a native of Zion.’”


## Psalms 87:5 (id: 10019087005)

5People will say about Zion, “Every one of those people is a native of that city,” and God Most High will personally keep Zion strong.


## Psalms 87:6 (id: 10019087006)

6When Yahweh registers each of those people, he will record, “This person is a native of Zion.” Selah


## Psalms 87:7 (id: 10019087007)

7Singers and musicians {will celebrate and say}, “Zion, everything that makes me flourish comes from you!”


## Psalms 88:0 (id: 10019088000)

0One of the Korahites wrote this song. This is a note for the worship leader: The tune is “Weakness of Afflicting.” Heman the Ezrahite wrote this song.


## Psalms 88:1 (id: 10019088001)

1Yahweh, you are the God who saves me. I continually ask you to help me.


## Psalms 88:2 (id: 10019088002)

2Please pay attention to what I am saying as I pray to you.


## Psalms 88:3 (id: 10019088003)

3I ask this because I have experienced so many bad things that I cannot endure any more; I am about to die and go to the place where dead people are.


## Psalms 88:4 (id: 10019088004)

4People expect me to die soon because I have so little strength left.


## Psalms 88:5 (id: 10019088005)

5It is as if I belong among dead people who are lying in their graves. It seems that you are no longer aware of such people and you stop taking care of them.


## Psalms 88:6 (id: 10019088006)

6It is as though you have thrown me down to the bottom of a deep, dark pit.


## Psalms 88:7 (id: 10019088007)

7I feel as if you are angry with me, and that is like an oppressive burden to me. It is as if I am drowning in your anger. Selah


## Psalms 88:8 (id: 10019088008)

8Because my illness is so unpleasant and you have not healed me, my friends stay away from me, and I am not able to leave my home {to visit anyone}.


## Psalms 88:9 (id: 10019088009)

9My eyes are swollen because I cry so much. Yahweh, throughout the day I call out to you for help as I spread out my hands toward you {in prayer}.


## Psalms 88:10 (id: 10019088010)

10If I were to die, it would be too late for you to do a miracle to help me. Dead people do not come back to life and praise you! Selah


## Psalms 88:11 (id: 10019088011)

11Dead bodies in graves do not talk about how you faithfully love us. In the place where dead people go, no one talks about how faithful you are.


## Psalms 88:12 (id: 10019088012)

12No one in that dark place sees the amazing things you do; no one in the place where no one remembers anything knows how good you are.


## Psalms 88:13 (id: 10019088013)

13But I {am still alive,} Yahweh, and I am calling out to you to help me. It is as if my prayer is already there waiting for you when you get up in the morning.


## Psalms 88:14 (id: 10019088014)

14Yahweh, please do not reject me! Please do not ignore me!


## Psalms 88:15 (id: 10019088015)

15Since I was young, I have been so ill that I have nearly died. I have been sick for so long with this terrible illness that you permit that I have no more hope.


## Psalms 88:16 (id: 10019088016)

16I feel as if you are very angry with me. This terrible illness that you permit has destroyed the good life I could have had.


## Psalms 88:17 (id: 10019088017)

17The bad things that are happening to me are like floodwaters that continually surround me and are about to drown me.


## Psalms 88:18 (id: 10019088018)

18Because you have allowed this illness, my friends and family members stay away from me. I can no longer see where they are.


## Psalms 89:0 (id: 10019089000)

0Ethan the Ezrahite wrote this song.


## Psalms 89:1 (id: 10019089001)

1Yahweh, I will always sing about how faithfully you love your people. I will tell my children and grandchildren that you always do what you promise.


## Psalms 89:2 (id: 10019089002)

2I know that you will always love us faithfully. Just as the sky is permanent, so you will always be faithful to us.


## Psalms 89:3 (id: 10019089003)

3Yahweh has said, “I made a covenant with the man I chose {to be the king of Israel}. That was David, who loyally obeyed me. I made a solemn promise to him.


## Psalms 89:4 (id: 10019089004)

4I said, ‘I will make sure that your descendants always rule as kings; yes, I will make sure that your dynasty never ends.’” Selah


## Psalms 89:5 (id: 10019089005)

5Yahweh, the angels in heaven praise you for the wonderful things you do, When those holy beings gather, they celebrate how you always do what you promise.


## Psalms 89:6 (id: 10019089006)

6No one in the sky is equal to Yahweh! None of the heavenly beings is like Yahweh!


## Psalms 89:7 (id: 10019089007)

## Psalms 89:26 (id: 10019089026)

## Psalms 89:27 (id: 10019089027)

## Psalms 89:29 (id: 10019089029)

## Psalms 89:30 (id: 10019089030)

## Psalms 89:33 (id: 10019089033)

## Psalms 89:34 (id: 10019089034)

## Psalms 89:35 (id: 10019089035)

## Psalms 89:36 (id: 10019089036)

## Psalms 89:37 (id: 10019089037)

## Psalms 89:38 (id: 10019089038)

## Psalms 89:39 (id: 10019089039)

## Psalms 89:40 (id: 10019089040)

## Psalms 89:43 (id: 10019089043)

## Psalms 89:44 (id: 10019089044)

## Psalms 89:45 (id: 10019089045)

## Psalms 89:46 (id: 10019089046)

## Psalms 89:47 (id: 10019089047)

## Psalms 89:48 (id: 10019089048)

## Psalms 89:49 (id: 10019089049)

## Psalms 89:51 (id: 10019089051)

## Psalms 89:52 (id: 10019089052)

## Psalms 90:17 (id: 10019090017)

## Psalms 101:1 (id: 10019101001)

1David wrote this psalm. Yahweh, I will praise you with singing! I will sing that you are just and that you faithfully love us.


## Psalms 101:2 (id: 10019101002)

2I will examine how to live in the right way. Yahweh, I need you to come and help me! In everything that I do in my household, I will sincerely try to do what is right.


## Psalms 101:3 (id: 10019101003)

3I will not tempt myself to do any bad thing. I hate any kind of sinful action; I will not allow myself to think about doing those things.


## Psalms 101:4 (id: 10019101004)

## Psalms 101:5 (id: 10019101005)

## Psalms 101:6 (id: 10019101006)

## Psalms 101:7 (id: 10019101007)

## Psalms 101:8 (id: 10019101008)

## Psalms 102:0 (id: 10019102000)

0Someone wrote this prayer when he was feeling discouraged and pleads for Yahweh to help him.


## Psalms 102:1 (id: 10019102001)

1Yahweh, please listen to me when I pray to you; yes, please be attentive as I cry out to you!


## Psalms 102:2 (id: 10019102002)

2When distressing things happen to me, please do not ignore me! Listen to me, and answer me quickly as soon as I call out to you!


## Psalms 102:3 (id: 10019102003)

3Please hurry because my life is ending as quickly as smoke that disappears; my body feels like a fire is burning it.


## Psalms 102:4 (id: 10019102004)

4I feel as though I am withering away like grass that the sun has scorched. You see, I do not even remember to eat any food.


## Psalms 102:5 (id: 10019102005)

5I groan aloud so much that my bones protrude under my skin.


## Psalms 102:6 (id: 10019102006)

6I am {lonely and despised} like an owl in the desert; I have become like a screech owl in the ruins {of a town}.


## Psalms 102:7 (id: 10019102007)

7I lie awake {at night}; I am like a lonely bird sitting on a housetop.


## Psalms 102:8 (id: 10019102008)

8During the whole of each day my enemies insult me. Those who make fun of me use my name when they curse people, saying, “May you be like him.”


## Psalms 102:9-10 (id: 10019102009)

9\-10It feels as though you have picked me up and thrown me away! Because you are so very angry with me, my food tastes like ashes and my tears fall into my drink.


## Psalms 102:11 (id: 10019102011)

## Psalms 102:12 (id: 10019102012)

## Psalms 102:13 (id: 10019102013)

## Psalms 102:14 (id: 10019102014)

14It is true that we who serve you love everything about Jerusalem.


## Psalms 102:15 (id: 10019102015)

15Yahweh, someday the people of all nations will revere you, and all the kings on the earth will recognize how very glorious you are.


## Psalms 102:16 (id: 10019102016)

16You see, it is Yahweh who builds Jerusalem; he will appear {there} gloriously.


## Psalms 102:17 (id: 10019102017)

17Yahweh listens to the prayers of {his} people who are homeless. He does not ignore them when they pray for him to help them.


## Psalms 102:18 (id: 10019102018)

18I will write these things down so that people in future years will know {what he has done}, {in order that} people who are not born yet will praise Yahweh.


## Psalms 102:19 (id: 10019102019)

19{They will describe} how Yahweh looked down from his holy place in heaven and saw {what was happening on} the earth.


## Psalms 102:20 (id: 10019102020)

20Yahweh looked down in order to help the groaning prisoners. He looked down in order to set free the people whom their enemies were about to kill.


## Psalms 102:21 (id: 10019102021)

21As a result, people in Jerusalem will tell what Yahweh has done and praise him for it.


## Psalms 102:22 (id: 10019102022)

22This will happen when many peoples, including those from other kingdoms, gather to worship Yahweh together.


## Psalms 102:23 (id: 10019102023)

23{But now} he has caused me to become weak while I am still young; {I think that} I will not remain alive very long.


## Psalms 102:24 (id: 10019102024)

24I say {to you}, “My God, do not let me die when I am only half\-way through my lifetime! In contrast, you live forever!


## Psalms 102:25 (id: 10019102025)

25You created the world long ago, and you are the one who made the heavens.


## Psalms 102:26 (id: 10019102026)

26As for the earth and the heavens, they will disappear, but as for you, you will remain. All of it will wear out as clothes wear out. You will get rid of them as people get rid of old clothes, and they will no longer exist.


## Psalms 102:27 (id: 10019102027)

27But you are Yahweh; you will always be.


## Psalms 102:28 (id: 10019102028)

28The children of those who worship you will live safely {in Jerusalem}, and you will protect their descendants as they live in your presence.”


## Psalms 103:1 (id: 10019103001)

1A psalm of David I tell myself, “Praise Yahweh!” To everything that is within me, I say, “Praise him because he is holy!”


## Psalms 103:2 (id: 10019103002)

2I tell myself, “Praise Yahweh, and think about all of the kind things that he has done for me.”


## Psalms 103:3 (id: 10019103003)

3He forgives all of my sins, and he heals me from all of my sicknesses.


## Psalms 103:4 (id: 10019103004)

4He rescues me from things that would kill me; he blesses me by faithfully loving me and acting mercifully to me.


## Psalms 103:5 (id: 10019103005)

5He gives me plenty of good things {during my entire life}. He makes me feel young {and strong} {as though I could fly high} like an eagle.


## Psalms 103:6 (id: 10019103006)

6It is Yahweh who does all this! He does what is right and judges justly for all those whom people have been treating unfairly.


## Psalms 103:7 (id: 10019103007)

7{Long ago} he revealed to Moses the way that he works; he showed to {our ancestors,} the people of Israel, the {mighty} things that he can do.


## Psalms 103:8 (id: 10019103008)

8Yahweh acts mercifully and kindly; he does not quickly get angry {when we sin}; he always faithfully loves us.


## Psalms 103:9 (id: 10019103009)

9He will not always rebuke us, and he will not remain angry forever.


## Psalms 103:10 (id: 10019103010)

10He does not punish us for our sins nearly as much as we deserve.


## Psalms 103:11 (id: 10019103011)

11Indeed, as high as the sky is above the earth, that is how much Yahweh faithfully loves everyone who reveres him.


## Psalms 103:12 (id: 10019103012)

12He has taken away the guilt for our sins and put it as far from us as the east is from the west.


## Psalms 103:13 (id: 10019103013)

13Just as parents always love their children, Yahweh always loves those who revere him.


## Psalms 103:14 (id: 10019103014)

14Indeed, he certainly knows how weak we are; he remembers that he created us from dust.


## Psalms 103:15 (id: 10019103015)

15The lifetime of humans passes quickly, like that of grass. We are like wild flowers that bloom for a short while,


## Psalms 103:16 (id: 10019103016)

16but then the wind blows over them and they disappear, and no one knows they were ever there.


## Psalms 103:17 (id: 10019103017)

17But Yahweh will continue to faithfully love all those who revere him, forever. He will do what is right for generations of their children,


## Psalms 103:18 (id: 10019103018)

18that is, he will act that way to all those who obey the covenant that he made with them. Yes, he will act that way to all those who obey what he has commanded us to do.


## Psalms 103:19 (id: 10019103019)

19Yahweh rules as king in heaven. From there, he rules over everything.


## Psalms 103:20 (id: 10019103020)

20Praise Yahweh, you angels who belong to Yahweh, You powerful warriors who do what he tells you to do, you who obey what he commands!


## Psalms 103:21 (id: 10019103021)

21Praise Yahweh, all you armies of his angels, you who serve him and do what he desires!


## Psalms 103:22 (id: 10019103022)

22All of the creatures that Yahweh has created should praise him! They should praise him in every place where he rules, {which is everywhere}! I say to myself, “Praise Yahweh!”


## Psalms 104:1 (id: 10019104001)

1I tell myself, “Bless Yahweh!” Yahweh, my God, you are magnificent! You are more majestic than a king appearing in his beautiful robes!


## Psalms 104:2 (id: 10019104002)

2You use light {itself} as your robe; you spread out {all of} heaven as your tent.


## Psalms 104:3 (id: 10019104003)

3The rain clouds are like the upper rooms of your palace. The clouds are like chariots for you; the wind is like a bird that takes you wherever you want to go.


## Psalms 104:4 (id: 10019104004)

4You send the winds like your messengers, {and you control} fire as your servant.


## Psalms 104:5 (id: 10019104005)

5You created the world to be secure, so that nothing can ever move it from its place.


## Psalms 104:6 (id: 10019104006)

6Later, you covered the earth with a flood, like covering someone with clothing; water even covered the mountains.


## Psalms 104:7 (id: 10019104007)

## Psalms 104:8 (id: 10019104008)

## Psalms 104:9 (id: 10019104009)

9Then you set a boundary for the water that it cannot cross over. That boundary ensures that the water will never again cover the whole earth.


## Psalms 104:10 (id: 10019104010)

10You make the springs that pour water into the stream beds; their water flows between the mountains.


## Psalms 104:11 (id: 10019104011)

## Psalms 104:12 (id: 10019104012)

## Psalms 104:13 (id: 10019104013)

## Psalms 104:14 (id: 10019104014)

## Psalms 104:15 (id: 10019104015)

15You give us {grapes that produce} wine that makes us cheerful; you give us {olives that produce} oil to make our faces shine, and you give us {grain to make} bread that makes us strong.


## Psalms 104:16 (id: 10019104016)

16Yahweh, you send plenty of rain to water your cedar trees that you planted in Lebanon.


## Psalms 104:17 (id: 10019104017)

17Birds make their nests in those trees, and storks make their nests in the cypress trees.


## Psalms 104:18 (id: 10019104018)

18High up in the mountains is where the wild goats live, and hyraxes live in the rocky cliffs.


## Psalms 104:19 (id: 10019104019)

19Yahweh, you made the moon to indicate the times for our festivals, and you made the sun to always go down at the right time.


## Psalms 104:20 (id: 10019104020)

20You bring darkness, and it becomes night when all of your forest animals prowl around.


## Psalms 104:21 (id: 10019104021)

21The young lions roar then as they seek their prey, looking for the food that you have provided for them.


## Psalms 104:22 (id: 10019104022)

## Psalms 104:23 (id: 10019104023)

## Psalms 104:24 (id: 10019104024)

24Yahweh, you have made so many different kinds of things! You were very wise as you made them all. The earth is full of the creatures that you made.


## Psalms 104:25 (id: 10019104025)

25We see the ocean, which is vastly wide between its shores! It is full of many kinds of wriggling creatures, so many that no one can count them. There are creatures ranging from small to big.


## Psalms 104:26 (id: 10019104026)

26Ships sail on the sea. Leviathan is also there, the creature that you made to splash around in it.


## Psalms 104:27 (id: 10019104027)

27All of these creatures depend on you to give them the food that they need at the right time.


## Psalms 104:28 (id: 10019104028)

28When you give them the food that they need, then they gather it. You give it to them freely. They eat until they are full of your good things.


## Psalms 104:29 (id: 10019104029)

29But when you refuse to give food to them, they panic. When you cause them to stop breathing, they die; their bodies decay and become part of the soil.


## Psalms 104:30 (id: 10019104030)

30When you cause {newborn} creatures to begin to breathe, they begin to live. You give new life to {all the living creatures on} the earth.


## Psalms 104:31 (id: 10019104031)

31I want all of Yahweh’s creatures to glorify him forever! I want Yahweh to rejoice about everything that he has created!


## Psalms 104:32 (id: 10019104032)

32He makes the earth shake just by looking at it! When he {merely} touches the mountains they start to burn!


## Psalms 104:33 (id: 10019104033)

33I will sing to Yahweh as long as I live. I will praise my God until the day that I die.


## Psalms 104:34 (id: 10019104034)

34May Yahweh be pleased by all {these things} that I have thought about him. As for me, I will rejoice because I know him.


## Psalms 104:35 (id: 10019104035)

35However, I want sinners to disappear from the earth, and for there to be no more wicked people! I tell myself, “Bless Yahweh!” Praise Yahweh!


## Psalms 105:1 (id: 10019105001)

1Give thanks to Yahweh; worship him and pray to him. Tell everyone in the world what he has done!


## Psalms 105:2 (id: 10019105002)

2Sing to him; praise him with instruments; tell others about all of his wonderful miracles!


## Psalms 105:3 (id: 10019105003)

3Be proud of Yahweh, who is the holy one. You people who worship Yahweh, rejoice!


## Psalms 105:4 (id: 10019105004)

4Ask Yahweh to help you and to make you strong; always try to be with him!


## Psalms 105:5-6 (id: 10019105005)

5\-6You people who are descendants of Yahweh’s servant Abraham, you descendants of Jacob, the people whom Yahweh has chosen, think about all of the wonderful things that he has done; he performed miracles, and he proclaimed {righteous} judgments.


## Psalms 105:7 (id: 10019105007)

7He is Yahweh, the God whom we worship. He rules over people throughout the earth.


## Psalms 105:8 (id: 10019105008)

8He never forgets the covenant that he made; that is, he will maintain what he promised for countless generations.


## Psalms 105:9 (id: 10019105009)

9That is the covenant that he made with Abraham, and that he repeated with an oath to Isaac.


## Psalms 105:10 (id: 10019105010)

10Then he repeated it as a reliable covenant with Jacob, that is, with Israel, and it was a covenant that would last forever.


## Psalms 105:11 (id: 10019105011)

11What Yahweh said to them was, “I will give to you the region of Canaan; that will be what you {and your descendants} inherit from me.”


## Psalms 105:12 (id: 10019105012)

12Yahweh said that to the people of Israel when there were only a few of them. They were just a tiny group of people who were living in that land like strangers.


## Psalms 105:13 (id: 10019105013)

13They continually wandered from one place to another, living in various kingdoms and people groups.


## Psalms 105:14 (id: 10019105014)

14But Yahweh did not allow others to oppress the people of Israel. On their behalf, he warned kings {by saying to them},


## Psalms 105:15 (id: 10019105015)

15“Do not harm the people whom I have chosen! Do nothing bad to my prophets!”


## Psalms 105:16 (id: 10019105016)

16Yahweh created a famine in the land of Canaan, that is, all of the people there had no food to eat.


## Psalms 105:17 (id: 10019105017)

17{So the people of Israel went to Egypt, but} before they went, Yahweh had already sent someone {there}. {He sent} Joseph, whom his brothers had sold to be a slave.


## Psalms 105:18 (id: 10019105018)

18{Later, while Joseph was in prison in Egypt,} the guards trapped his feet in shackles, and they put an iron collar around his neck.


## Psalms 105:19 (id: 10019105019)

19{Joseph was in prison} until the time when the events that he had predicted happened. Yahweh spoke to him {during this time} and taught Joseph to trust him more.


## Psalms 105:20 (id: 10019105020)

20The king of Egypt, the ruler of the people, commanded his servants who went to the prison and set Joseph free.


## Psalms 105:21 (id: 10019105021)

21Then the king chose Joseph as the one to decide about everything in the king’s household, that is, to be in charge of everything that the king possessed.


## Psalms 105:22 (id: 10019105022)

22The king permitted Joseph to command the king’s officials to do anything that Joseph wanted them to do, and Joseph taught the king’s advisors how to be wise.


## Psalms 105:23 (id: 10019105023)

23{Later, Joseph’s father} Jacob, also called Israel, arrived in Egypt. He lived like a foreigner in that land that belonged to the descendants of Ham.


## Psalms 105:24 (id: 10019105024)

24Over the years, Yahweh caused the descendants of Jacob to become very numerous. As a result, the enemies of the people of Israel considered that the people of Israel were becoming too strong.


## Psalms 105:25 (id: 10019105025)

25So Yahweh caused the rulers of Egypt to hate Yahweh’s people, and they devised ways to oppress his people.


## Psalms 105:26 (id: 10019105026)

26But then Yahweh sent his servant Moses along with {Moses’ older brother} Aaron, whom Yahweh had also chosen {to serve him}.


## Psalms 105:27 (id: 10019105027)

27Those two performed Yahweh’s amazing miracles among the people of Egypt in that land where the descendants of Ham lived.


## Psalms 105:28 (id: 10019105028)

28Yahweh sent darkness so that the land of Egypt was dark, and Moses and Aaron obeyed what Yahweh told them to do.


## Psalms 105:29 (id: 10019105029)

29Yahweh caused all of the water in Egypt to become blood, which caused all of their fish to die.


## Psalms 105:30 (id: 10019105030)

30Then he caused the land to become full of frogs; the royal family even had frogs in their bedrooms!


## Psalms 105:31 (id: 10019105031)

31Then Yahweh commanded {that flies come}, and {swarms of} flies descended {on the people of Egypt}. Gnats also swarmed across the whole country.


## Psalms 105:32 (id: 10019105032)

32Yahweh sent them hail instead of rain, and he started fires that burned throughout their land.


## Psalms 105:33 (id: 10019105033)

33The hail ruined their grapevines, fig trees, and destroyed all the other trees in their country.


## Psalms 105:34 (id: 10019105034)

34Yahweh commanded locusts to come, and hordes of them came of different kinds; so many came that no one could count them.


## Psalms 105:35 (id: 10019105035)

35The locusts ate every green plant in their land, including all of the crops.


## Psalms 105:36 (id: 10019105036)

36Then Yahweh killed every firstborn son, the one who first demonstrated that the man was virile, {in every house} of the people of Egypt.


## Psalms 105:37 (id: 10019105037)

37Then Yahweh brought the Israelite people out {from Egypt}. They were carrying {many heavy things made of} silver and gold {that the people of Egypt had given to them}, and yet none of his people stumbled as they went.


## Psalms 105:38 (id: 10019105038)

38The people of Egypt were glad when the Israelite people left because the people of Egypt had become very afraid of the Israelites.


## Psalms 105:39 (id: 10019105039)

39{Then} Yahweh spread a cloud to cover the Israelites; and at night {it became} a {big} fire {in the sky} to give them light.


## Psalms 105:40 (id: 10019105040)

40{Then} the Israelites asked {for meat to eat}, and Yahweh sent {flocks of} quail to them, and he gave them plenty of {manna} bread from the sky {each morning}.


## Psalms 105:41 (id: 10019105041)

41{One day} Yahweh split open a rock, and water poured out {from the opening for the people to drink}. The water became a river flowing in the desert.


## Psalms 105:42 (id: 10019105042)

42Yahweh did those things because he continually thought about the sacred promise that he had given to his servant, Abraham.


## Psalms 105:43 (id: 10019105043)

43So his people were joyful as Yahweh brought them out {from Egypt}. These people whom he had chosen were shouting joyfully as they went.


## Psalms 105:44 (id: 10019105044)

44Then Yahweh gave to the people of Israel the land that had belonged to the people groups {that lived there in Canaan}. So the Israelites took over the fields that those peoples had planted.


## Psalms 105:45 (id: 10019105045)

45Yahweh did all these things so that his people would do all the things that he had commanded them to do. Praise Yahweh!


## Psalms 106:1 (id: 10019106001)

1Praise Yahweh! Thank Yahweh because he is good and because he faithfully loves us forever!


## Psalms 106:2 (id: 10019106002)

2Because Yahweh has done so many great things, no one can tell all the great things that Yahweh has done, and no one can praise him enough.


## Psalms 106:3 (id: 10019106003)

3How happy are those who act fairly, always doing what is right!


## Psalms 106:4 (id: 10019106004)

4Yahweh, be kind to me when you help your people! Include me when you rescue them!


## Psalms 106:5 (id: 10019106005)

5Allow me to see the people whom you have chosen become prosperous again. Allow me to be happy with the people of your nation when they become happy again. Allow me to praise you along with all those others who belong to you.


## Psalms 106:6 (id: 10019106006)

6We have sinned just as our ancestors did; we have done all kinds of evil.


## Psalms 106:7 (id: 10019106007)

7Our ancestors did not appreciate the wonderful things that you did when they were in Egypt. They forgot about the many times that you showed that you faithfully loved them. Instead, when they were at the Sea of Reeds, they rebelled against you.


## Psalms 106:8 (id: 10019106008)

8Even so, he rescued them because of who he is, so that he could show that he is very powerful.


## Psalms 106:9 (id: 10019106009)

9He rebuked the Sea of Reeds and it became dry. Then he led our ancestors through it and even the deepest parts were as dry as a desert.


## Psalms 106:10 (id: 10019106010)

10In that way he rescued them from their hateful enemies.


## Psalms 106:11 (id: 10019106011)

11Then their enemies drowned under the water {of the Sea of Reeds}; not one of them remained {alive}.


## Psalms 106:12 (id: 10019106012)

12When that happened, our ancestors believed that Yahweh had truly done for them what he had promised to do, and they sang to praise him.


## Psalms 106:13 (id: 10019106013)

13But they soon forgot what he had done for them; they did things without waiting to find out what Yahweh wanted them to do.


## Psalms 106:14 (id: 10019106014)

14They intensely wanted certain foods in the wilderness. They did evil things there that challenged God to punish them.


## Psalms 106:15 (id: 10019106015)

15So he gave them what they requested, but then he made many of them sick so that they died.


## Psalms 106:16 (id: 10019106016)

16Later when some of the men in the camp became resentful of Moses and of {his older brother} Aaron, who was dedicated to serve Yahweh {by being a priest},


## Psalms 106:17 (id: 10019106017)

17the ground opened up and Dathan fell into it and it also buried Abiram and his people.


## Psalms 106:18 (id: 10019106018)

18God made a fire start among their people that burned up all the wicked people {who agreed with them}.


## Psalms 106:19 (id: 10019106019)

19Then the Israelite leaders made a metal statue of a calf at Mount Horeb and they worshiped it.


## Psalms 106:20 (id: 10019106020)

20So instead of worshiping their glorious God, they worshiped a statue of a bull that eats grass!


## Psalms 106:21 (id: 10019106021)

21They ignored God, who had rescued them by the great miracles that he performed in Egypt.


## Psalms 106:22 (id: 10019106022)

22They stopped thinking about the wonderful things that he did for them in Egypt and the amazing things that he did for them at the Sea of Reeds.


## Psalms 106:23 (id: 10019106023)

23Because of that, God said that he would get rid of the Israelites; but Moses, whom God had chosen {to serve him}, confronted God to persuade him not to destroy {them}.


## Psalms 106:24 (id: 10019106024)

24Then our ancestors refused to enter the beautiful land {of Canaan} because they did not believe that God would fulfill his promise {that he would enable them to take the land from the people who were living there}.


## Psalms 106:25 (id: 10019106025)

25Instead, they stayed in their tents and complained. They did not obey what Yahweh said that they should do.


## Psalms 106:26 (id: 10019106026)

26So Yahweh solemnly swore to them that he would cause them to die there in the wilderness,


## Psalms 106:27 (id: 10019106027)

27and that he would spread their descendants among the people of other nations, yes, that he would disperse them in those {foreign} lands.


## Psalms 106:28 (id: 10019106028)

28Later the Israelite people began worshiping the statue of Baal at Mount Peor, and they ate meat that had been sacrificed to dead {ancestors}.


## Psalms 106:29 (id: 10019106029)

29When the people did these things, that made Yahweh angry, so {again} he caused many of them to get a terrible disease.


## Psalms 106:30 (id: 10019106030)

30But Phinehas stood up and punished {the ones who had sinned}, and as a result the plague ended.


## Psalms 106:31 (id: 10019106031)

31Because of what Phinehas did, people have considered him to be a righteous man, and people in all future years will continue to remember him as righteous.


## Psalms 106:32 (id: 10019106032)

32Then at Meribah, where the water came out {of the rock}, our ancestors caused Yahweh to become angry {again}, and Moses suffered because of what they did.


## Psalms 106:33 (id: 10019106033)

33Moses suffered because they caused him to become very angry, and he said things that were foolish.


## Psalms 106:34 (id: 10019106034)

34Our ancestors did not destroy the other people groups as Yahweh had told them to do.


## Psalms 106:35 (id: 10019106035)

35Instead, the men married women from those people groups, and they started doing the {evil} things that those people did.


## Psalms 106:36 (id: 10019106036)

36Our ancestors worshiped the idols of those people, and that is how they began to reject Yahweh and his ways.


## Psalms 106:37 (id: 10019106037)

37{Many of} the Israelites sacrificed their sons and daughters to the demons {that those idols represented}.


## Psalms 106:38 (id: 10019106038)

38They killed their own sons and daughters, who had done nothing wrong, by offering them as sacrifices to the idols of the Canaanites. Because of those murders, the land of Canaan became defiled.


## Psalms 106:39 (id: 10019106039)

39Because of what they did they made it impossible for Yahweh to accept them. By worshiping idols instead of faithfully worshiping only Yahweh, they became like prostitutes.


## Psalms 106:40 (id: 10019106040)

40So Yahweh became very angry with his people; he was completely disgusted with them.


## Psalms 106:41 (id: 10019106041)

41As a result, he allowed other people groups to conquer our ancestors, so that those who hated our ancestors were their rulers.


## Psalms 106:42 (id: 10019106042)

42Their enemies oppressed our ancestors and completely controlled them.


## Psalms 106:43 (id: 10019106043)

43Many times Yahweh saved his people, but they insisted on rebelling against him, and they destroyed themselves by being more and more sinful.


## Psalms 106:44 (id: 10019106044)

44However, Yahweh saw everything that was distressing to them, and he listened to them when they cried out to him for help.


## Psalms 106:45 (id: 10019106045)

45He thought about the covenant that he had made with them, and because he was completely committed to faithfully loving them, he decided that he would stop {punishing them}.


## Psalms 106:46 (id: 10019106046)

46He caused all those who had captured the Israelites to feel sorry for them.


## Psalms 106:47 (id: 10019106047)

47Yahweh our God, rescue us and bring us back to Israel from among those other people groups so that we may thank you who are holy and joyfully praise you!


## Psalms 106:48 (id: 10019106048)

48Praise Yahweh, the God whom we Israelites worship! Praise him now and forever! Let all the people say, “We agree! Praise Yahweh!”


## Psalms 107:1 (id: 10019107001)

1Thank Yahweh because he is good and because he faithfully loves us forever!


## Psalms 107:2 (id: 10019107002)

2Those whom Yahweh has saved should tell others that he has rescued them from their enemies.


## Psalms 107:3 (id: 10019107003)

3He has gathered them together from {many foreign} lands; he has gathered them from the east and the west, from the north and from the {southern} sea.


## Psalms 107:4 (id: 10019107004)

4Some of those who returned from those countries wandered in the trackless desert; they could find no town to live in.


## Psalms 107:5 (id: 10019107005)

5They were hungry and thirsty, and they became weak and discouraged.


## Psalms 107:6 (id: 10019107006)

6Then because they were distressed, they called out to Yahweh, and he rescued them from what was distressing them.


## Psalms 107:7 (id: 10019107007)

7He led them along a straight road where they walked {safely} to cities where they could live.


## Psalms 107:8 (id: 10019107008)

8They should praise Yahweh for how he has loved them faithfully and for the wonderful things that he does for all people!


## Psalms 107:9 (id: 10019107009)

9They should thank him because he satisfies people who are thirsty, and he gives hungry people plenty of good food to eat.


## Psalms 107:10 (id: 10019107010)

10Some of them sat in deep darkness; they were prisoners, suffering in iron {chains fastened to their hands and feet}.


## Psalms 107:11 (id: 10019107011)

11They were suffering in prison because they had rebelled against God; they actually rejected the instructions that the Most High God gave to them.


## Psalms 107:12 (id: 10019107012)

12God made them experience difficult things so that they would no longer be proud. When bad things happened to them, there was no one who would help them.


## Psalms 107:13 (id: 10019107013)

13Then because they were so distressed, they called out to Yahweh, and he rescued them from what was distressing them.


## Psalms 107:14 (id: 10019107014)

14He broke the chains that were on their hands and feet and brought them out of those very dark prisons.


## Psalms 107:15-16 (id: 10019107015)

15\-16Yahweh broke down the bronze gates and he cut through the iron bars of their prison. Therefore they should praise Yahweh for how he has loved them faithfully and for the wonderful things that he does for all people!


## Psalms 107:17 (id: 10019107017)

17Some of them foolishly rebelled {against God}, and they suffered because of their sins.


## Psalms 107:18 (id: 10019107018)

18They did not want to eat any food, and they almost died.


## Psalms 107:19 (id: 10019107019)

19Then because they were so distressed, they called out to Yahweh, and he rescued them from what was distressing them.


## Psalms 107:20 (id: 10019107020)

20Then Yahweh healed them by speaking a message of healing; in this way he saved them from dying.


## Psalms 107:21 (id: 10019107021)

21They should praise Yahweh for how he has loved them faithfully and for the wonderful things that he does for all people!


## Psalms 107:22 (id: 10019107022)

22They should give offerings to him to show that they are thankful, and they should tell about the miracles that he has performed by singing joyfully!


## Psalms 107:23 (id: 10019107023)

23Some of them sailed in ships; they bought and sold things far away across the sea.


## Psalms 107:24 (id: 10019107024)

24These people saw the creatures that Yahweh created; they saw the amazing things that he had made {as they traveled} across the very deep seas.


## Psalms 107:25 (id: 10019107025)

25Then he commanded the winds to become strong. They became a storm and stirred up very high waves.


## Psalms 107:26 (id: 10019107026)

26The ships in which they were sailing went up on the high waves, then they went down into the troughs between the waves. This dangerous action terrified the sailors.


## Psalms 107:27 (id: 10019107027)

27They stumbled about and staggered as drunken men do, and everything that they tried to do was useless.


## Psalms 107:28 (id: 10019107028)

28Then because they were distressed, they called out to Yahweh, and he rescued them from what was distressing them.


## Psalms 107:29 (id: 10019107029)

29He calmed the storm and he stilled the waves.


## Psalms 107:30 (id: 10019107030)

30Then they were very glad that the storm had become calm and Yahweh brought them {safely} into the harbor where they had wanted to go.


## Psalms 107:31 (id: 10019107031)

31They should praise Yahweh for how he has loved them faithfully and for the wonderful things that he does for all people!


## Psalms 107:32 (id: 10019107032)

32They should praise him among the Israelite people when they have gathered together, and they should praise him in front of the leaders of the community.


## Psalms 107:33 (id: 10019107033)

33Yahweh causes rivers to become dry so that the land becomes barren, and he makes springs of water to become dusty ground.


## Psalms 107:34 (id: 10019107034)

34Yahweh causes land that normally produces a lot of crops to become salty wastelands. He does that because the people who live there are very wicked.


## Psalms 107:35 (id: 10019107035)

35But Yahweh also creates pools of water in deserts, that is, he causes springs to flow where the ground had been dry.


## Psalms 107:36 (id: 10019107036)

36Then he brings people who are hungry into that land, to live there and to build cities there.


## Psalms 107:37 (id: 10019107037)

37Then they sow {some of} the fields with seeds and {in other fields} they plant grapevines and it all produces {a large amount of} crops.


## Psalms 107:38 (id: 10019107038)

38He blesses those people so that they have very many children, and so that their herds of cattle become very large.


## Psalms 107:39 (id: 10019107039)

39Then the people become fewer because the leaders oppress them and cause them to become distressed and to suffer.


## Psalms 107:40 (id: 10019107040)

40Then Yahweh shows that he despises the leaders {who oppress them}, and makes those leaders wander in a wilderness where there are no roads.


## Psalms 107:41 (id: 10019107041)

41But he rescues poor people from those who oppress them and causes {their} families to increase in number as flocks of sheep do.


## Psalms 107:42 (id: 10019107042)

42People who live rightly see {Yahweh do these things}, and they rejoice; wicked people {know about these things, too,} {but they} have nothing to say in reply.


## Psalms 107:43 (id: 10019107043)

43Those who are wise should think carefully about these things; they should thoughtfully consider all the things that Yahweh has done to show that he faithfully loves them.


## Psalms 108:0 (id: 10019108000)

0David wrote this psalm. It is a song.


## Psalms 108:1 (id: 10019108001)

1God, I am completely committed to {worshiping} you. I will sing and I will play music {to praise you}. I will do this with my whole being!


## Psalms 108:2 (id: 10019108002)

2I will arise before the sun rises, and I will praise you while I play my lyre and my harp.


## Psalms 108:3 (id: 10019108003)

3Yahweh, I will thank you as foreign people listen; yes, I will play music to praise you before other people groups.


## Psalms 108:4 (id: 10019108004)

4I will do that because the way that you faithfully love us is so great that it is like the distance between the heavens and the earth, and the way that you faithfully keep your promises is so great that it is like the distance between the earth and the clouds.


## Psalms 108:5 (id: 10019108005)

5God, show {all creatures} that you are greater than anything in the heavens! Show how wonderful you are {to people} all over the earth!


## Psalms 108:6 (id: 10019108006)

6Please answer our prayers and rescue us from our enemies by your power so that we, the people whom you love, may be safe.


## Psalms 108:7 (id: 10019108007)

7Because God is holy, he answered, “I will joyfully divide the area of Shechem {for you} and I will distribute {among my people} the land in the Valley of Succoth.


## Psalms 108:8 (id: 10019108008)

8{The region of} Gilead is mine; {the area of the tribe of} Manasseh is mine; {the tribe of} Ephraim is {like} my war helmet; and {the tribe of} Judah is {like} my royal scepter.


## Psalms 108:9 (id: 10019108009)

9The people of Moab serve me as those who wash my feet; the people of Edom also belong to me as menial servants; I have defeated the people of Philistia.”


## Psalms 108:10 (id: 10019108010)

10I wait for God to lead us to {fight against the people of} Edom; I wait for God to lead my army to {attack} their {capital} city that has strong walls around it.


## Psalms 108:11 (id: 10019108011)

11God, we hope that you have not abandoned us; {we are distressed that} you have not been going with us when our army marches out {to fight our enemies}.


## Psalms 108:12 (id: 10019108012)

12Please help us when we fight against our enemies because the help that humans can give {to us} is worthless.


## Psalms 108:13 (id: 10019108013)

13{But} with you {helping us}, we will win; you are the one who will {enable us to} defeat our enemies.


## Psalms 109:1 (id: 10019109001)

1A note to the worship leader: David wrote this psalm. God, you are the one whom I praise, so please answer my prayer


## Psalms 109:2 (id: 10019109002)

2because evil and dishonest people slander me and tell lies about me.


## Psalms 109:3 (id: 10019109003)

3On all sides of me they say things that show that they hate me, and they harm me for no reason.


## Psalms 109:4 (id: 10019109004)

4I show them that I love them but instead of being kind to me, they say that I have done evil things. So I pray to God {about it}.


## Psalms 109:5 (id: 10019109005)

5I do good things for them and they respond by doing evil things to me. I love them and they respond by hating me.


## Psalms 109:6 (id: 10019109006)

6So appoint a wicked judge who will judge my enemy, and bring one {of his enemies} who will stand next to him and accuse him {of doing evil things}.


## Psalms 109:7 (id: 10019109007)

7Let that judge declare that my enemy is guilty, and may you judge that even his prayer {to you} is a sin.


## Psalms 109:8 (id: 10019109008)

8Then cause him to die soon; let people honor someone else rather than him.


## Psalms 109:9 (id: 10019109009)

9Cause his children not to have a father anymore, and cause his wife to become a widow.


## Psalms 109:10 (id: 10019109010)

10Cause his children to wander far from their ruined homes, begging and looking for food.


## Psalms 109:11 (id: 10019109011)

11Let the people to whom he owed money seize all of his property; let strangers take away everything that he worked to acquire.


## Psalms 109:12 (id: 10019109012)

12Make sure that no one is faithfully loving to him; make sure that no one pities his fatherless children.


## Psalms 109:13 (id: 10019109013)

13Cause all his children to die, so that there will not be another generation to continue his name.


## Psalms 109:14 (id: 10019109014)

14Yahweh, consider all of the evil things that his ancestors did and do not forgive the sins that his mother committed.


## Psalms 109:15 (id: 10019109015)

15Please think about their sins always, but cause everyone on earth to completely forget who they were.


## Psalms 109:16 (id: 10019109016)

16I pray these things because that man{, my enemy,} was never faithful toward anyone as your covenant says we must be; instead, he persecuted poor, needy, and grieving people until they were dead.


## Psalms 109:17 (id: 10019109017)

17He enjoyed cursing {other people}, so now people are cursing him. He did not want to bless {other people}, so there will be no one who blesses him.


## Psalms 109:18 (id: 10019109018)

18Cursing {other people} is as natural for him as wearing clothes. Cursing {other people} is so much a part of who he is that it is like the water that he drinks; it is so much a part of who he is that it is like olive oil that has soaked into his bones.


## Psalms 109:19 (id: 10019109019)

19Cause those terrible curses to continually afflict him like clothes that cling to him and like the belt that he wears every day.


## Psalms 109:20 (id: 10019109020)

20Yahweh, let this {curse} be the way that you punish all of those who accuse me {of doing bad things} and all of those who say evil things about me.


## Psalms 109:21 (id: 10019109021)

21But you, Yahweh, my God, please be kind to me because of who you are! Rescue me {from my enemies} because you faithfully love me as you promised.


## Psalms 109:22 (id: 10019109022)

22I ask you to do this because I am destitute and I am intensely sorrowful.


## Psalms 109:23 (id: 10019109023)

23I think that my time to remain alive will end as quickly as an evening shadow disappears. I will be gone as quickly as a man shakes off a locust.


## Psalms 109:24 (id: 10019109024)

24My knees are weak because I have fasted very often, and my body has become very thin; any fat is gone.


## Psalms 109:25 (id: 10019109025)

25The people who accuse me despise me; when they see me, they insult me by shaking their heads at me.


## Psalms 109:26 (id: 10019109026)

26Yahweh, the God whom I worship, please help me! Rescue me because you faithfully love me!


## Psalms 109:27 (id: 10019109027)

27Then my enemies will know that you, Yahweh, are the one who has done it!


## Psalms 109:28 (id: 10019109028)

28As for them, they will curse {me}, but you, in contrast, will bless {me}. When they persecute {me}, {the result will be that} you make them ashamed, but you cause {me,} your servant, to be glad!


## Psalms 109:29 (id: 10019109029)

29Please let everyone see that those who accuse me are completely disgraceful! Yes, cause other people to see that they are shameful as easily as someone sees the clothes that they wear!


## Psalms 109:30 (id: 10019109030)

30I will thank Yahweh very much out loud; I will praise him when I am among the crowd of people who are worshiping him.


## Psalms 109:31 (id: 10019109031)

31I will do that because he defends poor people {like me} in order to save {us} from those who would condemn us to die.


## Psalms 110:1 (id: 10019110001)

1David wrote this psalm. Yahweh said to my lord, “Sit {here,} close to me, in the place of highest honor until I completely defeat your enemies and make them {like} a stool for your feet!”


## Psalms 110:2 (id: 10019110002)

2Yahweh will extend your powerful kingdom from Zion {to other lands}; you are to rule over all your enemies.


## Psalms 110:3 (id: 10019110003)

3When you lead your army into battle, your people will offer themselves freely {to join your army}. The way that they are completely devoted to you will be beautiful. You will be as strong as you were as a young man every day, as surely as the dew comes that waters the earth every morning.


## Psalms 110:4 (id: 10019110004)

4Yahweh has made a solemn promise and he will never change his mind {about it}; {he said to the king,} “You are an eternal priest in the way that Melchizedek was a priest.”


## Psalms 110:5 (id: 10019110005)

5The Lord fights for you; when he becomes angry, he defeats many kings.


## Psalms 110:6 (id: 10019110006)

6He will punish the people of many nations; he will cause many {enemy soldiers} to fall dead, with crushed heads, all over a broad land.


## Psalms 110:7 (id: 10019110007)

7But the king will drink from the stream alongside the road; because of {all} this, he will be victorious.


## Psalms 111:1 (id: 10019111001)

1Praise Yahweh! I will thank Yahweh with my entire inner being every time that those who do what is right meet together.


## Psalms 111:2 (id: 10019111002)

2The things that Yahweh does are wonderful! All those who delight in those things study them carefully.


## Psalms 111:3 (id: 10019111003)

3The things that he does show how wonderful and glorious he is, and that he is eternally righteous.


## Psalms 111:4 (id: 10019111004)

4He has done wonderful things that people will always remember; Yahweh always acts kindly and mercifully.


## Psalms 111:5 (id: 10019111005)

5He provides food for those who profoundly respect him; he never forgets the covenant that he made {with our ancestors}.


## Psalms 111:6 (id: 10019111006)

6By enabling his people to capture the lands that had belonged to other people groups, he showed to {us,} his people, that he is very powerful.


## Psalms 111:7 (id: 10019111007)

7He does everything fairly and in the right way, and we can trust that everything that he tells us to do is for our good.


## Psalms 111:8 (id: 10019111008)

8We must obey his instructions forever; he created them in order to do what is best for us, and they are completely right.


## Psalms 111:9 (id: 10019111009)

9He rescued us, his people, {from being slaves in Egypt,} and he made a covenant {with us} that will last forever. He is holy and awesome!


## Psalms 111:10 (id: 10019111010)

10Having a profound respect for Yahweh is the way to become wise. All those who obey his instructions will come to understand why it is good to do them. We should praise him forever!


## Psalms 112:1 (id: 10019112001)

1Praise Yahweh! How fortunate are those who have a profound respect for Yahweh; How fortunate are those who happily obey his commands!


## Psalms 112:2 (id: 10019112002)

2Their descendants will prosper in their land; Yahweh will bless all who do what is right.


## Psalms 112:3 (id: 10019112003)

3Their families will be wealthy, and their righteous deeds will endure forever.


## Psalms 112:4 (id: 10019112004)

4For those who honor God, it is as if a light were shining on them in the darkness, on those who are kind, merciful, and righteous.


## Psalms 112:5 (id: 10019112005)

5It is good to be kind to others and to generously lend money to them. A person like that does everything honestly.


## Psalms 112:6 (id: 10019112006)

6Righteous people do this because they know that bad things will never overwhelm them; other people will always {joyfully} remember them.


## Psalms 112:7 (id: 10019112007)

7Righteous people are not afraid when they receive bad news; they confidently trust in Yahweh.


## Psalms 112:8 (id: 10019112008)

8They are confident and not afraid because they know that they will see God defeat their enemies.


## Psalms 112:9 (id: 10019112009)

9They give things generously to poor people; their kind deeds will endure forever; people will greatly honor them.


## Psalms 112:10 (id: 10019112010)

10Wicked people will see {those things} and become angry; they will gnash their teeth {angrily}, then people will stop hearing from them and they will die. The wicked things that they want to do will never happen.


## Psalms 113:1 (id: 10019113001)

1Praise Yahweh! You people who serve Yahweh, praise him! Praise Yahweh for who he is!


## Psalms 113:2 (id: 10019113002)

2Everyone should praise Yahweh for who he is, both now and forevermore!


## Psalms 113:3 (id: 10019113003)

3From the people who live in the far east to the people who live in the far west, everyone should praise Yahweh for who he is!


## Psalms 113:4 (id: 10019113004)

4Yahweh rules over all the nations, he is more glorious than anything under the heavens.


## Psalms 113:5 (id: 10019113005)

5There is no one who is like Yahweh, the God whom we worship, who sits on his throne in the highest heaven


## Psalms 113:6 (id: 10019113006)

6and who stoops down to look at the sky and at the earth.


## Psalms 113:7 (id: 10019113007)

7He rescues very poor people from the very poor way that they live


## Psalms 113:8 (id: 10019113008)

8and causes people to honor them by making them leaders among those who rule his people.


## Psalms 113:9 (id: 10019113009)

9He also enables women who have had no children to live in their houses as happy mothers with children. Praise Yahweh!


## Psalms 114:1 (id: 10019114001)

1When the Israelite people left Egypt, yes, when the descendants of Jacob left those people who spoke a foreign language,


## Psalms 114:2 (id: 10019114002)

2the land of Judah became the place where people worshiped God; and the land of Israel became the area that he ruled over.


## Psalms 114:3 (id: 10019114003)

3{When they came to} the Sea {of Reeds}, {it was as though the water} saw {them} and ran away! {When they came to} the Jordan River, {the water in the river} stopped flowing {so that the Israelites could cross it}.


## Psalms 114:4 (id: 10019114004)

4{When Mount Sinai shook,} {it was as though} the mountains skipped like rams and the hills jumped like lambs.


## Psalms 114:5 (id: 10019114005)

5{If someone asks,} “What happened at the Sea {of Reeds} that made it seem to run away? What happened that caused {the water in} the Jordan {River} to stop flowing?


## Psalms 114:6 (id: 10019114006)

6{What happened at Mount Sinai that made the} mountains {seem to} skip like rams and made the hills {seem to} jump like lambs?”


## Psalms 114:7 (id: 10019114007)

7{I would reply,} “The earth trembles because the Lord is there! Everyone should tremble because of the presence of the God whom Jacob worshiped!”


## Psalms 114:8 (id: 10019114008)

8He is the one who caused pools of water to flow from a rock {for the Israelite people to drink}; indeed, he caused a spring to flow from a solid rock!


## Psalms 115:0 (id: 10019115000)

0A psalm written by David


## Psalms 115:1 (id: 10019115001)

1Yahweh, {we ask} not so people will honor us, no, not that at all; rather, we want people to honor you alone because of seeing how you faithfully love us and by seeing you do what you have promised to do.


## Psalms 115:2 (id: 10019115002)

2It is not right that other people groups should say {about us}, “We do not see their God doing anything {to help them}.”


## Psalms 115:3 (id: 10019115003)

3Well, the God whom we worship lives in heaven; he does whatever he wants!


## Psalms 115:4 (id: 10019115004)

4But the idols of those people groups are only silver and gold {statues}; humans made them.


## Psalms 115:5 (id: 10019115005)

5Their idols have mouths, but they cannot say anything; they have eyes, but they cannot see anything.


## Psalms 115:6 (id: 10019115006)

6They have ears, but they cannot hear anything; they have noses, but they cannot smell anything.


## Psalms 115:7 (id: 10019115007)

7They have hands, but they cannot feel anything; they have feet, but they cannot walk; they cannot even make any sounds in their throats!


## Psalms 115:8 (id: 10019115008)

8The people who make those idols are {as powerless} as those idols, Everyone who trusts in those idols {to do anything} is also {powerless}!


## Psalms 115:9 (id: 10019115009)

9You, my fellow Israelite people, trust in Yahweh! He is the one who helps you and protects you like a shield.


## Psalms 115:10 (id: 10019115010)

10You {priests, the} descendants of Aaron, trust in Yahweh! He is the one who helps you and protects you like a shield.


## Psalms 115:11 (id: 10019115011)

11All you who deeply respect Yahweh, trust in Yahweh! He is the one who helps you and protects you like a shield.


## Psalms 115:12 (id: 10019115012)

12Yahweh has not forgotten us; he will bless {us}. he will bless us Israelite people! He will bless the priests!


## Psalms 115:13 (id: 10019115013)

13Yahweh will bless all those who deeply respect him— those who are less important as well as the important people, that is, each one!


## Psalms 115:14 (id: 10019115014)

14I pray that Yahweh may give {many children} {both} to you, {my fellow Israelite people,} and to your children {after you}.


## Psalms 115:15 (id: 10019115015)

15I pray that Yahweh, the one who made heaven and the earth, may bless all of you!


## Psalms 115:16 (id: 10019115016)

16The highest heavens belong to Yahweh, but he gave everything that is on the earth to humankind.


## Psalms 115:17 (id: 10019115017)

17People who are dead are not able to praise Yahweh; when they go to the place where dead people are, they become silent and cannot praise him.


## Psalms 115:18 (id: 10019115018)

18But we who are alive will praise Yahweh, both now and forevermore. Praise Yahweh!


## Psalms 116:1 (id: 10019116001)

1I love Yahweh because he hears me when I cry out for him to help me.


## Psalms 116:2 (id: 10019116002)

2I love him because he listens to me, so I will call out to him all during my life.


## Psalms 116:3 (id: 10019116003)

3Everything around me caused me to think that I was about to die; it seemed to me that I was already dying. I was distressed and very sad.


## Psalms 116:4 (id: 10019116004)

4But then I called out to Yahweh. I said, “Yahweh, I plead with you to save me!”


## Psalms 116:5 (id: 10019116005)

5Yahweh is kind and does what is right. He is the God whom we worship, and he loves us.


## Psalms 116:6 (id: 10019116006)

6Yahweh protects those who are helpless; when I was experiencing bad things, he saved me.


## Psalms 116:7 (id: 10019116007)

7I encourage myself to relax once again because Yahweh has restored me.


## Psalms 116:8 (id: 10019116008)

8Indeed, Yahweh has saved me from dying, he has delivered me from being sorrowful, and he has kept me from making bad mistakes.


## Psalms 116:9 (id: 10019116009)

9So while I am still alive, I will live as Yahweh directs me.


## Psalms 116:10 (id: 10019116010)

10I continued to trust {in Yahweh}, even when I said, “As for me, I am greatly suffering.”


## Psalms 116:11 (id: 10019116011)

11For my part, I was so distressed that I thought, “I cannot trust anyone.”


## Psalms 116:12 (id: 10019116012)

12I can never repay Yahweh for all the good things that he has done for me.


## Psalms 116:13 (id: 10019116013)

13{But I can do this:} I will present a cup {of wine as a thank offering} as I call out to thank him for saving me.


## Psalms 116:14 (id: 10019116014)

14Also, as the people who belong to Yahweh watch, I will give to him the offerings that I solemnly promised to give to him.


## Psalms 116:15 (id: 10019116015)

15Yahweh is very grieved when one of his people dies.


## Psalms 116:16 (id: 10019116016)

16Certainly I am one of Yahweh’s people; I am his servant; I have belonged to him since my mother bore me. So he saved me from what was going to kill me.


## Psalms 116:17 (id: 10019116017)

17So I will offer to him a sacrifice to thank him, and I will pray to Yahweh.


## Psalms 116:18-19 (id: 10019116018)

18\-19As the people who belong to Yahweh watch, in the courtyard outside his temple, in the middle of Jerusalem, I will give to him the offerings that I solemnly promised to give to him. Praise Yahweh!


## Psalms 117:1 (id: 10019117001)

1You people of all nations, praise Yahweh! All you people groups, greatly praise him!


## Psalms 117:2 (id: 10019117002)

2Do this, because he is fully committed to love us faithfully, just as he promised to do, and we can trust that Yahweh will be faithful forever. Praise Yahweh!


## Psalms 118:1 (id: 10019118001)

1Thank Yahweh because he is good and because he faithfully loves us forever!


## Psalms 118:2 (id: 10019118002)

2All Israelite people should repeatedly shout, “because he faithfully loves us forever!”


## Psalms 118:3 (id: 10019118003)

3You {priests} who are descendants of Aaron should repeatedly shout, “because he faithfully loves us forever!”


## Psalms 118:4 (id: 10019118004)

4All you who deeply revere Yahweh should repeatedly shout, “because he faithfully loves us forever!”


## Psalms 118:5 (id: 10019118005)

5When I was distressed, I called out to Yahweh, and Yahweh answered me and set me free.


## Psalms 118:6 (id: 10019118006)

6Yahweh is on my side, so I will not be afraid of anything. I know that humans cannot really harm me!


## Psalms 118:7 (id: 10019118007)

7Yes, Yahweh is on my side. He helps me defeat my enemies, so I know that I will look {triumphantly} on them.


## Psalms 118:8 (id: 10019118008)

8It is better to rely on Yahweh than to depend on people.


## Psalms 118:9 (id: 10019118009)

9It is better to rely on Yahweh than to trust that influential people will protect us.


## Psalms 118:10 (id: 10019118010)

10Armies of many nations surrounded me, but Yahweh powerfully enabled me to make them retreat.


## Psalms 118:11 (id: 10019118011)

11They completely surrounded me, but Yahweh powerfully enabled me to make them retreat.


## Psalms 118:12 (id: 10019118012)

12They swarmed around me like {angry} bees, but they diminished as quickly as a fire that is burning only thorns; Yahweh powerfully enabled me to make them retreat.


## Psalms 118:13 (id: 10019118013)

13My enemies attacked me fiercely and thought that they would defeat me, but Yahweh helped me.


## Psalms 118:14 (id: 10019118014)

14Yahweh is the one who makes me strong, and who gives me a reason to sing; and he saves me from my enemies.


## Psalms 118:15 (id: 10019118015)

15Listen to the joyful songs of how Yahweh saves people! They come from the tents of the people who honor God! They sing, “Yahweh has powerfully defeated our enemies;


## Psalms 118:16 (id: 10019118016)

16Yahweh has shown himself to be victorious! Yahweh has powerfully defeated our enemies!”


## Psalms 118:17 (id: 10019118017)

17My enemies will not kill me; no, I will live to proclaim the great things that Yahweh has done.


## Psalms 118:18 (id: 10019118018)

18Yahweh has punished me severely, but he has not allowed me to die.


## Psalms 118:19 (id: 10019118019)

19{You gatekeepers,} open for me the gates through which those who obey Yahweh may enter, so that I may enter through them and thank Yahweh.


## Psalms 118:20 (id: 10019118020)

20{The gatekeeper says,} “This is Yahweh’s gate; all who obey him may enter through it.”


## Psalms 118:21 (id: 10019118021)

21{Yahweh,} I thank you that you answered my prayer and saved me {from my enemies}.


## Psalms 118:22 (id: 10019118022)

22{People considered me worthless, like} a stone that builders rejected, {but then Yahweh showed that he highly values me, as if} that stone then became the most important stone in the building.


## Psalms 118:23 (id: 10019118023)

23The people said, “Yahweh made that happen, and for us, it is a wonderful thing.


## Psalms 118:24 (id: 10019118024)

24Yahweh made possible everything that we are celebrating today; we should be extremely happy about all of it!


## Psalms 118:25 (id: 10019118025)

25Yahweh, we plead with you to keep rescuing us {from our enemies}. Yahweh, please continue to help us to succeed {against our enemies}.


## Psalms 118:26 (id: 10019118026)

26Yahweh, bless the one who will come with your power. From the temple we bless all of you.


## Psalms 118:27 (id: 10019118027)

27Yahweh is God, and he has made us victorious. Bring an animal sacrifice {for him} and tie it to the horns of the altar.”


## Psalms 118:28 (id: 10019118028)

28Yahweh, you are the God whom I worship, and I will praise you! You are the only God whom I worship, and I will tell everyone that you are great!


## Psalms 118:29 (id: 10019118029)

29Thank Yahweh because he is good and because he faithfully loves us forever!


## Psalms 119:1 (id: 10019119001)

1How fortunate are those about whom no one can say truthfully that they have done things that are wrong! Those are the people who always obey Yahweh’s instructions.


## Psalms 119:2 (id: 10019119002)

2How fortunate are those who do what he tells them to do! Those are the people who sincerely want to know him.


## Psalms 119:3 (id: 10019119003)

3They certainly do not want to cheat other people; they live in a way that pleases Yahweh.


## Psalms 119:4 (id: 10019119004)

4Yahweh, you have given us your instructions so that we would carefully obey them.


## Psalms 119:5 (id: 10019119005)

5I want very much to continually obey everything that you command!


## Psalms 119:6 (id: 10019119006)

6If I did that, then I would not be ashamed when I think about everything that you command.


## Psalms 119:7 (id: 10019119007)

7As I learn all of your just regulations, I thank you sincerely.


## Psalms 119:8 (id: 10019119008)

8I will obey your regulations; do not desert me!


## Psalms 119:9 (id: 10019119009)

9I know how a young person can live in the right way; it is by obeying your commands.


## Psalms 119:10 (id: 10019119010)

10I eagerly want to please you. Do not allow me to disobey what you have commanded.


## Psalms 119:11 (id: 10019119011)

## Psalms 119:12 (id: 10019119012)

12Yahweh, I praise you; teach me your rules.


## Psalms 119:13 (id: 10019119013)

13I have proclaimed to the people all of the things that you have commanded us to do.


## Psalms 119:14 (id: 10019119014)

14I delight in obeying your instructions; I enjoy that more than being very rich.


## Psalms 119:15 (id: 10019119015)

15I will carefully think about everything that you have commanded me, yes, I will pay attention to the way of living that you have taught me.


## Psalms 119:16 (id: 10019119016)

16I am happy to obey your decrees; I will not forget what you have said.


## Psalms 119:17 (id: 10019119017)

17Do good things for me, the one who serves you, in order that I may continue to live and obey you.


## Psalms 119:18 (id: 10019119018)

18Help me to understand how wonderful your instructions are.


## Psalms 119:19 (id: 10019119019)
