## Psalms 119:20 (id: 10019119020)

20I am weak from constantly desiring to know your instructions.


## Psalms 119:21 (id: 10019119021)

21You rebuke people who are proud; you curse people who disobey your commands.


## Psalms 119:22 (id: 10019119022)

22Do not allow them to continue to insult and scorn me; {I request this} because I have obeyed what you have required.


## Psalms 119:23 (id: 10019119023)

23Rulers gather together and plan ways to harm me, but I, your servant, will meditate on what you have commanded me.


## Psalms 119:24 (id: 10019119024)

24Yes, I am delighted with your requirements; they are like {good} advisors for me.

25{Sometimes} I feel like I will soon die; allow me to live as you have told me that you would do.


## Psalms 119:26 (id: 10019119026)

## Psalms 119:27 (id: 10019119027)

27Help me to understand how you want me to behave, and then I can think about how amazing your instructions are.


## Psalms 119:28 (id: 10019119028)

28I am very sad, with the result that I am becoming weak; enable me to be strong again through a message from you.


## Psalms 119:29 (id: 10019119029)

29Prevent me from telling lies, and be kind to me by teaching me your instructions.


## Psalms 119:30 (id: 10019119030)

30I have decided that I will faithfully obey you; I know that what you say is right.


## Psalms 119:31 (id: 10019119031)

31Yahweh, I try to carefully obey what you say; please do not allow me to do anything shameful.


## Psalms 119:32 (id: 10019119032)

32I eagerly obey your instructions because you help me increasingly to understand {how they benefit me}.


## Psalms 119:33 (id: 10019119033)

33Yahweh, teach me what your regulations mean, and then I will always obey them.


## Psalms 119:34 (id: 10019119034)

34Help me to understand your instructions so that I may eagerly obey them.


## Psalms 119:35 (id: 10019119035)

35I am happy to do the things that you command, so teach me the best way to obey them.


## Psalms 119:36 (id: 10019119036)

36Cause me to want to do what you say rather than to want to get things {that belong to other people}.

38Please do what you promised to do for me, your servant. Your promises are what cause people to deeply revere you.

43Please do allow me to truthfully tell {them how you saved me}; you see, I am patiently waiting for you to act justly.


## Psalms 119:44 (id: 10019119044)

44I will consistently obey your instructions, for as long as I live.


## Psalms 119:45 (id: 10019119045)

45I will live freely and safely because I {always} try to obey your regulations.

47I am very happy to obey the things that you command me to do; I love to do them.


## Psalms 119:48 (id: 10019119048)

48I eagerly ask to receive more of your commandments. I love them, and I will think deeply about everything that you require us to do.

## Psalms 119:50 (id: 10019119050)

50When I am suffering, your promise to me comforts me; knowing that you will do what you have said makes me joyful again in living {for you}.


## Psalms 119:51 (id: 10019119051)

51Proud people are always making fun of me, but I do not stop obeying your instructions.


## Psalms 119:52 (id: 10019119052)

52Yahweh, I think about your regulations that you gave to us long ago. Doing that comforts me.

## Psalms 119:54 (id: 10019119054)

63I am a friend of all those who deeply revere you, yes, to all those who obey what you tell us to do.

64Yahweh, you faithfully love people all over the earth; please {continue to} teach me how you want people to live.

65Yahweh, you have done good things for me, your servant, just as you promised that you would do.


## Psalms 119:66 (id: 10019119066)

66Teach me to understand {what is happening around me} and to think carefully {before I decide what to do}, because I believe that the best way to live is to obey what you tell us to do.

67Before you afflicted me, I did things that were wrong, but now I obey what you say to do.


## Psalms 119:68 (id: 10019119068)

69Proud people tell many lies about me, but as for me, I enthusiastically obey what you have told us to do.


## Psalms 119:70 (id: 10019119070)

70Those people are stupid, but as for me, I happily obey your instructions.

75Yahweh, I know that when you judge people, you are always right. I know that you have made me suffer only because you faithfully love me.

109My enemies are constantly trying to kill me, but I do not forget your instructions.

## Psalms 120:1 (id: 10019120001)

1A psalm for people going up to the temple to worship. When I was distressed, I called out to Yahweh, and he answered me.


## Psalms 120:2 (id: 10019120002)

2{I prayed,} “Yahweh, rescue me from people who tell lies, who say things to deceive me!”


## Psalms 120:3 (id: 10019120003)

## Psalms 120:4 (id: 10019120004)

## Psalms 120:5 (id: 10019120005)

## Psalms 120:6 (id: 10019120006)

6I have lived too long among people who hate to live peacefully with others.


## Psalms 120:7 (id: 10019120007)

## Psalms 121:1 (id: 10019121001)

1A psalm for people going up to the temple to worship. I look toward the hills {and I ask myself,} “Where will anyone to help me come from?”


## Psalms 121:2 (id: 10019121002)

## Psalms 121:5 (id: 10019121005)

## Psalms 121:7 (id: 10019121007)

## Psalms 121:8 (id: 10019121008)

## Psalms 122:1 (id: 10019122001)

1A psalm for people going up to the temple to worship. This is a psalm of David. I was very happy when people said to me, “We should go to Yahweh’s temple {in Jerusalem}!”


## Psalms 122:2 (id: 10019122002)

## Psalms 122:3 (id: 10019122003)

## Psalms 122:6 (id: 10019122006)

## Psalms 122:7 (id: 10019122007)

## Psalms 122:9 (id: 10019122009)

## Psalms 123:1 (id: 10019123001)

1A psalm for people going up to the temple to worship. Yahweh, I look up toward you who live in heaven.


## Psalms 123:2 (id: 10019123002)

## Psalms 123:3 (id: 10019123003)

3Yahweh, please be very merciful to us, because people have despised us so much that we cannot endure any more.


## Psalms 123:4 (id: 10019123004)

## Psalms 124:1 (id: 10019124001)

1A psalm for people going up to the temple to worship; of David. If Yahweh had not been helping us— All Israelites should repeat this—


## Psalms 124:2 (id: 10019124002)

2If Yahweh had not been helping us when our enemies attacked us,


## Psalms 124:3 (id: 10019124003)

3then they would have destroyed us completely, because they were so angry with us.


## Psalms 124:4 (id: 10019124004)

4They would have overwhelmed us like a flood; they would have swept over us like a raging river.


## Psalms 124:5 (id: 10019124005)

5{They would have destroyed us as completely as} raging waters would have drowned us.


## Psalms 124:6 (id: 10019124006)

## Psalms 124:7 (id: 10019124007)

## Psalms 124:8 (id: 10019124008)

8Yahweh is the one who helps us; he is the one who made everything that exists.


## Psalms 125:1 (id: 10019125001)

1A psalm for people going up to the temple to worship. People who trust in Yahweh are as secure as Mount Zion, which will stand firm forever.


## Psalms 125:2 (id: 10019125002)

2Just as the hills surrounding Jerusalem protect the city, so Yahweh protects his people, and he will protect them forever.


## Psalms 125:3 (id: 10019125003)

## Psalms 125:4 (id: 10019125004)

## Psalms 125:5 (id: 10019125005)

## Psalms 126:1 (id: 10019126001)

1A psalm for people going up to the temple to worship. When Yahweh brought us back to Zion after our enemies had captured us {and taken us away}, we felt as if we were dreaming.

## Psalms 126:4 (id: 10019126004)

## Psalms 126:5 (id: 10019126005)

## Psalms 126:6 (id: 10019126006)

## Psalms 127:1 (id: 10019127001)

1This is a psalm for people going up to the temple to worship. It is of Solomon. If people are building a house, but Yahweh does not approve of it, those who build it are doing nothing useful. If Yahweh does not protect a city, it is useless for the guards to stay awake watching it.


## Psalms 127:2 (id: 10019127002)

2It is useless for you to get up very early and stay up very late, working hard to get food, because Yahweh wants to give sleep to those whom he loves.


## Psalms 127:3 (id: 10019127003)

3Children are a gift that Yahweh gives; Offspring are a benefit {from him}.


## Psalms 127:4 (id: 10019127004)

## Psalms 127:5 (id: 10019127005)

## Psalms 128:1 (id: 10019128001)

1This is a psalm for those going up to the temple to worship. How good life is for all who deeply revere Yahweh and who do what he tells them to do!


## Psalms 128:2 (id: 10019128002)

2You will enjoy eating the food that you have worked hard for, and it will make you healthy.


## Psalms 128:3 (id: 10019128003)

## Psalms 128:4 (id: 10019128004)

4Yes, that is how Yahweh will bless every person who deeply reveres him.


## Psalms 128:5 (id: 10019128005)

## Psalms 128:6 (id: 10019128006)

## Psalms 129:1 (id: 10019129001)

1A psalm for people going up to the temple to worship. Ever since I was young, my enemies have oppressed me frequently. All of the people of Israel should say this {with me}:


## Psalms 129:2 (id: 10019129002)

2Ever since I was young, my enemies have oppressed me frequently, but they have never defeated me!


## Psalms 129:3 (id: 10019129003)

## Psalms 129:4 (id: 10019129004)

## Psalms 129:5 (id: 10019129005)

## Psalms 129:6 (id: 10019129006)

## Psalms 129:7 (id: 10019129007)

## Psalms 129:8 (id: 10019129008)

## Psalms 130:1 (id: 10019130001)

1This is a psalm for people going up to the temple to worship. Yahweh, I am very distressed, so I call out to you.

## Psalms 132:1 (id: 10019132001)

## Psalms 133:1 (id: 10019133001)

## Psalms 135:6 (id: 10019135006)

6Yahweh does whatever he wants to do, everywhere, whether it is in heaven or on the earth or in the ocean, even deep down in the ocean.


## Psalms 135:7 (id: 10019135007)

7He causes clouds to form in distant places on the earth; he makes lightning flash along with the rain, and he sends out wind from the places where he stores it.


## Psalms 135:8 (id: 10019135008)

8He killed every firstborn in Egypt, including those from both people and animals.


## Psalms 135:9 (id: 10019135009)

9He did amazing miracles among the people of Egypt to punish Pharaoh and all of his officials.


## Psalms 135:10 (id: 10019135010)

10He defeated many nations and killed their powerful kings.


## Psalms 135:11 (id: 10019135011)

11He killed Sihon, king of the Amorites, and Og, king of Bashan, and all the kings of Canaan.


## Psalms 135:12 (id: 10019135012)

12Then he gave the land that those kings had ruled over to his own people, that is, to {us,} the people of Israel, to be our own land.


## Psalms 135:13 (id: 10019135013)

13Yahweh, people will always know how great you are; Yahweh, people in every generation will remember you,


## Psalms 135:14 (id: 10019135014)

14because Yahweh defends his people and is merciful to those who serve him.


## Psalms 135:15 (id: 10019135015)

15The idols that other people groups worship are only statues made of silver and gold; people made them with their own hands.


## Psalms 135:16 (id: 10019135016)

16Those idols have mouths, but they cannot say anything; they have eyes, but they cannot see anything.


## Psalms 135:17 (id: 10019135017)

17They have ears, but they cannot hear anything; In fact, they do not even breathe.


## Psalms 135:18 (id: 10019135018)

18The people who make those idols will become as helpless as they are, and so will everyone who trusts in them.


## Psalms 135:19 (id: 10019135019)

19You Israelites, praise Yahweh! You descendants of Aaron, praise Yahweh!


## Psalms 135:20 (id: 10019135020)

20You descendants of Levi, praise Yahweh! All you who deeply revere Yahweh, praise him!


## Psalms 135:21 (id: 10019135021)

21Praise Yahweh from his temple on Mount Zion, where he lives in Jerusalem! Praise Yahweh!

## Psalms 136:16 (id: 10019136016)

16{Thank} him, the one who led his people {safely} through the desert, because he faithfully loves {us} forever!


## Psalms 136:17 (id: 10019136017)

17{Thank} him, the one who defeated powerful kings, because he faithfully loves {us} forever!


## Psalms 136:18 (id: 10019136018)

18He killed powerful kings, because he faithfully loves {us} forever!


## Psalms 136:19 (id: 10019136019)

19{He killed} Sihon, the king of the Amorites, because he faithfully loves {us} forever!

## Psalms 136:25 (id: 10019136025)

## Psalms 137:1 (id: 10019137001)

1{After soldiers had taken us to} Babylon, we sat down by the rivers there, and we cried when we thought about {the temple on Mount} Zion {in Jerusalem}.


## Psalms 137:2 (id: 10019137002)

2We hung our harps on the willow trees there {and did not play them}.


## Psalms 137:3 (id: 10019137003)

3We did that because those who had captured us {and taken us} there demanded that we sing for them. They tormented us and told us to sing a joyful song. {They said,} “Sing for us one of the songs about Zion!”


## Psalms 137:4 (id: 10019137004)

4{We thought}, “We are not able to sing {joyful} songs about Yahweh in this foreign land!”


## Psalms 137:5 (id: 10019137005)

5If I ever forget about Jerusalem, may my right hand be unable {to play the harp}!


## Psalms 137:6 (id: 10019137006)

6May my tongue and my mouth make me unable to sing if I forget about Jerusalem, or {even} if I do not consider that Jerusalem causes me to be more joyful than anything else does.


## Psalms 137:7 (id: 10019137007)

7Yahweh, please punish the Edomites for what they did when the enemy destroyed Jerusalem. They shouted, “Completely tear it down to its foundations!”


## Psalms 137:8 (id: 10019137008)

8Concerning the people of Babylon, who devastate {other nations}, How good it will be for the person who completely pays them back with what they did to us!


## Psalms 137:9 (id: 10019137009)

9How good it will be for the one who seizes your babies and smashes them against the rocks!


## Psalms 138:1 (id: 10019138001)

1This is a psalm of David. Yahweh, I sincerely thank you. In the presence of {idols of} other gods, I sing to praise you.


## Psalms 138:2 (id: 10019138002)

2I bow down toward your sacred temple {to worship you} and I thank you because you faithfully love us and because we can trust you to do all that you have promised. You have given cause for people everywhere to honor you and what you have said more than anything else.


## Psalms 138:3 (id: 10019138003)

3When I called out to you, you helped me; you enabled me to be brave and strong.


## Psalms 138:4 (id: 10019138004)

4Yahweh, {someday} all the kings of this earth should praise you because they will have heard what you have said.


## Psalms 138:5 (id: 10019138005)

5They should sing about what you have done because you are very glorious.


## Psalms 138:6 (id: 10019138006)

6Yahweh, although you live in heaven, you take care of humble people. Proud people, however, you identify from far away.


## Psalms 138:7 (id: 10019138007)

7Even when dangerous things happen all around me, you keep me safe. You rescue me from my enemies who are angry with me.


## Psalms 138:8 (id: 10019138008)

8Yahweh, I know that you will continue to do what is good for me; Yahweh, you faithfully love {us} forever. Please do not abandon {us,} the people that you have made!


## Psalms 139:1 (id: 10019139001)

1This is a note for the worship leader: This is a psalm of David. Yahweh, you have thoroughly examined me, and you know {me completely}.


## Psalms 139:2 (id: 10019139002)

2You are the one who knows everything that I do, including when I sit and when I stand. Even from far away, you know what I am thinking.


## Psalms 139:3 (id: 10019139003)

3You have examined in detail all of my movements, from the time I go out until I lie down, and you know intimately all of my habits.


## Psalms 139:4 (id: 10019139004)

4Indeed, even before I say anything, Yahweh, you already know everything that I am going to say!


## Psalms 139:5 (id: 10019139005)

5You guard me on every side, and you put your hand on me {to protect me}.


## Psalms 139:6 (id: 10019139006)

6I am in awe that you know everything about me. I am not able to comprehend that.


## Psalms 139:7 (id: 10019139007)

7There is nowhere I could go to get away from your Spirit! There is nowhere I could run to escape from your presence!


## Psalms 139:8 (id: 10019139008)

8If I went up to heaven, you would be there. Even if I lay down in the place where dead people go, you would be there!


## Psalms 139:9 (id: 10019139009)

9If I rose up and flew to where the sun rises in the east, or if I settled in the farthest place across the {western} sea,


## Psalms 139:10 (id: 10019139010)

10even there you would guide me and you would keep me secure.


## Psalms 139:11 (id: 10019139011)

11Even if I said, “Let the darkness completely overwhelm me, and the light around me turn to night,”


## Psalms 139:12 (id: 10019139012)

12even darkness is not dark to you, and the night shines as bright as the daytime. For you, darkness and light are the same.


## Psalms 139:13 (id: 10019139013)

13Indeed, you are the one who created my deepest inner parts; you formed me inside my mother’s womb.


## Psalms 139:14 (id: 10019139014)

14I praise you because you made me in such an awesome and wonderful way. Everything that you do is wonderful! I know that very well.


## Psalms 139:15 (id: 10019139015)

15You saw my bones when you made me in that hidden place. As you wove me together deep in the womb,


## Psalms 139:16 (id: 10019139016)

16you saw me before I had any form. You wrote in your book each day that you had planned for me to live, before any of those days had even started.


## Psalms 139:17 (id: 10019139017)

17God, the things that you think are very valuable to me! There are so many of them!


## Psalms 139:18 (id: 10019139018)

18If I tried to count them, {I would not be able to do that, because} they are more than the grains of sand {on the earth}. {Every morning} I wake up, I am still with you, {and there are still more of them to count}.


## Psalms 139:19 (id: 10019139019)

19God, I wish that you would kill all wicked people! I wish that all violent men would go away from me!


## Psalms 139:20 (id: 10019139020)

20They say wicked things about you and plan evil things; your enemies misuse your name.


## Psalms 139:21 (id: 10019139021)

21Yahweh, I certainly hate those who hate you, and I am disgusted with those who rise up against you.


## Psalms 139:22 (id: 10019139022)

22I hate them completely; I consider them to be my enemies.


## Psalms 139:23 (id: 10019139023)

23God, I invite you to search me and to know what I am thinking; please examine me and know what I worry about.


## Psalms 139:24 (id: 10019139024)

24See if there is any harmful way in me, and teach me to live in the way that lasts forever.


## Psalms 140:0 (id: 10019140000)

0This is a note to the worship leader: David wrote this psalm.


## Psalms 140:1 (id: 10019140001)

1Yahweh, please save me from evil people; protect me from people who want to attack me.


## Psalms 140:2 (id: 10019140002)

2They are always planning how to do evil things, and every day they provoke fights.


## Psalms 140:3 (id: 10019140003)

3They say things {to people} that hurt {them}; what comes from their mouths is as deadly as the venom of a viper. Selah


## Psalms 140:4 (id: 10019140004)

4Yahweh, protect me from wicked people who want to harm me; keep me safe from violent people who have planned to destroy me.


## Psalms 140:5 (id: 10019140005)

5Proud people are trying to destroy me. {It is as though} they have hidden a snare for me; {It is as though} they have spread out a net along my path; {It is as though} they have set traps to catch me. Selah


## Psalms 140:6 (id: 10019140006)

6I say to you, “Yahweh, you are my God.” Yahweh, please listen to me as I plead with you to help me!


## Psalms 140:7 (id: 10019140007)

7Yahweh, my Lord, you are the one who powerfully saves me; you have protected me during battles as though you had covered my head {with a helmet}.


## Psalms 140:8 (id: 10019140008)

8Yahweh, do not give wicked people what they want; do not let their plans succeed, or they will become proud. Selah


## Psalms 140:9 (id: 10019140009)

9As for the leaders of those who surround me, let the harmful things that they have said {to others} happen to them.


## Psalms 140:10 (id: 10019140010)

10Pour burning coals on them! Throw them into a fire! Throw them into deep holes from which they will never get out!


## Psalms 140:11 (id: 10019140011)

11Yahweh, please do not allow people who slander others to prosper in this land; Cause evil things to continually happen to violent people and destroy them.


## Psalms 140:12 (id: 10019140012)

12Yahweh, I know that you do what is just and right for poor people.


## Psalms 140:13 (id: 10019140013)

13People who do what is right will surely thank you, and they will live with you.


## Psalms 141:1 (id: 10019141001)

1This is a psalm of David. Yahweh, I call out to you; please help me quickly! Please listen to me when I am calling out to you.


## Psalms 141:2 (id: 10019141002)

2Please accept my prayer {as though it were} an offering of incense to you. Please accept me while I lift up my hands {to pray to you}, just as you accept the sacrifices that people offer to you each evening.


## Psalms 141:3 (id: 10019141003)

3Yahweh, do not allow me to say things that are bad or wrong. Do not allow me to say those things as a sentry does not allow the wrong people to go through a doorway.


## Psalms 141:4 (id: 10019141004)

4Prevent me from wanting to do anything that is wrong and from joining with wicked men when they want to do evil deeds. Do not even allow me to share in eating their delightful food!


## Psalms 141:5 (id: 10019141005)

5It is all right if righteous people strike me because they are acting faithfully and kindly toward me to correct me. {If they do that, it will be as if they have honored me by} anointing my head with {olive} oil, and may I never refuse to accept it. but I am always praying that you will punish the wicked because of the wicked deeds that they do.


## Psalms 141:6 (id: 10019141006)

6When their rulers are thrown down from the top of rocky cliffs, the wicked will hear what I am saying, and will know that what I am saying is right and good.


## Psalms 141:7 (id: 10019141007)

7{They will say,} “Just as one scatters {clods of earth} when he breaks up the ground, {soon} our bodies will lie scattered on the way to the place where dead people are.”


## Psalms 141:8 (id: 10019141008)

8But Lord Yahweh, I continue to rely on you. I ask you to protect me; please do not stop protecting my life!


## Psalms 141:9 (id: 10019141009)

9People who do evil things are trying to trick me and destroy me. It is as though they have laid out snares and set traps for me. Please keep me safe from what they are trying to do to me.


## Psalms 141:10 (id: 10019141010)

10I desire that wicked people fall together into the traps that they have set {to catch me}, while, for my part, I escape {from them}.


## Psalms 142:0 (id: 10019142000)

0This is a psalm that David prayed when he was hiding in a cave.


## Psalms 142:1 (id: 10019142001)

1Yahweh, I cry out to you; I plead for you to help me.


## Psalms 142:2 (id: 10019142002)

2I am complaining to you about {all of} my problems; I am telling you {all of} my troubles.


## Psalms 142:3 (id: 10019142003)

3When I am very discouraged, you are the one who knows what I must do. As I go about my activities, {it is as though} my enemies have hidden traps along my path for me {to fall into}.


## Psalms 142:4 (id: 10019142004)

4If you look to my right you will see that there is no one who watches over me. There is nowhere I can go to be safe, and no one cares what happens to me.


## Psalms 142:5 (id: 10019142005)

5So Yahweh, I called out to you {to help me}; I said, “You are the one who protects me; you are all that I need while I am alive.”


## Psalms 142:6 (id: 10019142006)

6Please listen to me while I call out to you {to help me} because I am very distressed. Save me from those who are making me suffer, because they are stronger than I am.


## Psalms 142:7 (id: 10019142007)

7Please free me from those who are distressing me so that I may thank you. When you do that, the other righteous people will gather with me {to praise you together} because you will have fulfilled my request.


## Psalms 143:1 (id: 10019143001)

1This is a psalm of David. Yahweh, please listen to me as I pray to you, pleading for you to help me! Because you are righteous and because you faithfully do what you have promised, please do what I am asking you to do for me!


## Psalms 143:2 (id: 10019143002)

2Please do not judge me, your servant, because you know that there is no one alive who is completely innocent.


## Psalms 143:3 (id: 10019143003)

3Please help me because my enemies have pursued me; they have completely defeated me. {It is as though} they have already put me in a grave like people who are dead for eternity.


## Psalms 143:4 (id: 10019143004)

4So I am very discouraged; I feel very dismayed.


## Psalms 143:5 (id: 10019143005)

5I remember what has happened previously; I think about all the things that you have done; I consider the {great} deeds that you have performed.


## Psalms 143:6 (id: 10019143006)

6I lift up my hands to you {as I pray}; I need you, just as much as a very dry land needs water. Selah


## Psalms 143:7 (id: 10019143007)

7Yahweh, I am absolutely despairing, so please answer me right now! Do not keep away from me because if you do that, I will {soon} be among those who descend to where the dead people are.


## Psalms 143:8 (id: 10019143008)

8Every morning cause me to remember that you faithfully love me because I trust in you. show me the things that I should do because I depend on you for my life.


## Psalms 143:9 (id: 10019143009)

9Yahweh, please save me from my enemies; I rely on you to protect me.


## Psalms 143:10 (id: 10019143010)

10You are the God to whom I belong, so teach me to do what pleases you. I want your good Spirit to show me the best way to live.


## Psalms 143:11 (id: 10019143011)

11Yahweh, please keep me alive because of who you are; because you are righteous, please rescue me from {the people who are} oppressing me!


## Psalms 143:12 (id: 10019143012)

12I am one who serves you; so because you faithfully love me as you promised to do, please kill all of my enemies who want to kill me.


## Psalms 144:1 (id: 10019144001)

1This is a psalm of David. I praise Yahweh, who protects me! He equips me to fight well in wars.


## Psalms 144:2 (id: 10019144002)

2He is always faithful and kind to me. He keeps me safe and helps me escape from danger, as if he were my shield or a place where I could go to be safe. He enables me to rule other peoples.


## Psalms 144:3 (id: 10019144003)

3Yahweh, people are so insignificant; it is amazing that you pay any attention to them!


## Psalms 144:4 (id: 10019144004)

4People live for only a very short time, just as a mist or a shadow quickly disappears.


## Psalms 144:5 (id: 10019144005)

5Yahweh, please come down from the sky and split open the mountains so that smoke pours from them.


## Psalms 144:6 (id: 10019144006)

6Please send lightning bolts like arrows against my enemies to make them run away.


## Psalms 144:7 (id: 10019144007)

7Reach down from heaven and rescue me from my enemies who are surrounding me like a dangerous flood. Save me from the power of these foreign people.


## Psalms 144:8 (id: 10019144008)

8They say things that are not true and raise their right hands to swear oaths that are false.


## Psalms 144:9 (id: 10019144009)

9God, I will sing a new song to you and play my ten\-stringed harp to praise you,


## Psalms 144:10 (id: 10019144010)

10because you are the one who saves kings and who rescues me, your servant David, from danger in battle.


## Psalms 144:11 (id: 10019144011)

11Save me from the power of these foreign people. They say things that are not true and raise their right hands to swear oaths that are false.


## Psalms 144:12 (id: 10019144012)

12Then our sons will be like strong plants that grow tall when they are young, and our daughters will be as beautiful and stately as pillars that craftsmen carve to decorate a palace.


## Psalms 144:13 (id: 10019144013)

13May our barns overflow with many kinds of crops. May our sheep give birth to great numbers of lambs, yes, very great numbers as they graze in our pastures.


## Psalms 144:14 (id: 10019144014)

14May our oxen be strong to pull plows. May no one break through our walls, may no one go into exile, and may no one cry out {in distress} in our city squares.


## Psalms 144:15 (id: 10019144015)

15What a good life people have when these things happen for them! What a good life people have who worship Yahweh as their God!


## Psalms 145:1 (id: 10019145001)

1This is a psalm of David. My God and king, I will proclaim that you are very great; I will praise you now and always.


## Psalms 145:2 (id: 10019145002)

2I will bless you every day; I will praise you forever.


## Psalms 145:3 (id: 10019145003)

3Yahweh, you are great, and people should praise you very much; no one can fully understand how great you are.


## Psalms 145:4 (id: 10019145004)

4People of every generation will tell the next generation about what you have done; they will tell about your mighty deeds.


## Psalms 145:5 (id: 10019145005)

5I will think about how very glorious and majestic you are, and I will think about your wonderful deeds.


## Psalms 145:6 (id: 10019145006)

6People will talk about how powerful your awesome deeds are, and I will proclaim how great you are.


## Psalms 145:7 (id: 10019145007)

7People will celebrate how very good you are, and they will sing joyfully about how righteous you are.


## Psalms 145:8 (id: 10019145008)

8Yahweh acts kindly and mercifully; he does not quickly become angry, and he faithfully loves us very much.


## Psalms 145:9 (id: 10019145009)

9Yahweh is good to everyone, and he acts mercifully toward everything that he has made.


## Psalms 145:10 (id: 10019145010)

10Yahweh, all the creatures that you have made will praise you, and the people who are faithful to you will bless you.


## Psalms 145:11 (id: 10019145011)

11They will tell others about how gloriously you rule as king and about how powerful you are,


## Psalms 145:12 (id: 10019145012)

12so that all people will know about your mighty deeds and about how gloriously and majestically you rule.


## Psalms 145:13 (id: 10019145013)

13You will never stop ruling as king; you will rule throughout every generation.


## Psalms 145:14 (id: 10019145014)

14Yahweh sustains people who are in difficult circumstances, and he helps those who are struggling.


## Psalms 145:15 (id: 10019145015)

15All creatures look to you expectantly, and you give them food when they need it.


## Psalms 145:16 (id: 10019145016)

16You give generously and satisfy the desire of every living thing.


## Psalms 145:17 (id: 10019145017)

17Yahweh does everything rightly; indeed, he acts faithfully in all that he does.


## Psalms 145:18 (id: 10019145018)

18Yahweh is ready to help everyone who asks him for help when they do that sincerely.


## Psalms 145:19 (id: 10019145019)

19He gives those who deeply respect him what they need. He responds to them when they call for help, and he rescues them.


## Psalms 145:20 (id: 10019145020)

20Yahweh protects all those who love him, but he destroys wicked people.


## Psalms 145:21 (id: 10019145021)

21I will always say things to praise Yahweh; I want all people always to praise him because he is so holy.


## Psalms 146:1 (id: 10019146001)

1Praise Yahweh! I will praise Yahweh with my whole being.


## Psalms 146:2 (id: 10019146002)

2I will praise Yahweh for as long as I am alive; Yes, I will sing to praise my God for the rest of my life.


## Psalms 146:3 (id: 10019146003)

3Since no human being can save you, do not rely on your leaders.


## Psalms 146:4 (id: 10019146004)

4When they die, their bodies decay and become soil again. When that happens, they can no longer carry out their plans.


## Psalms 146:5 (id: 10019146005)

5Some people confidently expect Yahweh their God to help them. He is the God whom Jacob worshiped. He does help them, and they have a very good life.


## Psalms 146:6 (id: 10019146006)

6He is the one who created the sky and the land. He also created the oceans and all the creatures that are in them. He always does what he has promised to do.


## Psalms 146:7 (id: 10019146007)

7He defends people whom others oppress, and he provides food for people who are hungry. Yahweh frees people who are in prison.


## Psalms 146:8 (id: 10019146008)

8Yahweh enables people who are blind to see again; Yahweh helps people who are struggling; Yahweh loves people who do what is right.


## Psalms 146:9 (id: 10019146009)

9Yahweh protects foreigners; he preserves the property of orphans and widows, but he makes the plans of wicked people fail.


## Psalms 146:10 (id: 10019146010)

10Yahweh will be the king forever; yes, the God of the people of Jerusalem will always reign. Praise Yahweh!


## Psalms 147:1 (id: 10019147001)

1Praise Yahweh! Singing praises to our God is a good thing to do. He deserves it, and it makes us happy.


## Psalms 147:2 (id: 10019147002)

2Yahweh is rebuilding Jerusalem and bringing back the Israelites whom our enemies had scattered.


## Psalms 147:3 (id: 10019147003)

3He comforts people who are very sad and heals their pain.


## Psalms 147:4 (id: 10019147004)

4He knows how many stars there are, and he has given each one its name.


## Psalms 147:5 (id: 10019147005)

5Our Lord is great and very powerful; no one can measure how much he understands.


## Psalms 147:6 (id: 10019147006)

6Yahweh helps humble people, but he makes wicked people fail in what they want to do.


## Psalms 147:7 (id: 10019147007)

7Thank Yahweh, our God, for all he has done for us as you play the harp and sing to him.


## Psalms 147:8 (id: 10019147008)

8He makes the sky form rain clouds so that they will rain on the earth and make grass grow on the mountains.


## Psalms 147:9 (id: 10019147009)

9He provides food for wild animals. He even feeds young ravens when they cry out for food.


## Psalms 147:10 (id: 10019147010)

10He does not value the strength of a horse or the speed of a human runner.


## Psalms 147:11 (id: 10019147011)

11Instead, Yahweh values people who deeply respect him. He values people who confidently expect him to be faithful to his covenant promises.


## Psalms 147:12 (id: 10019147012)

12Praise Yahweh our God, you people of Jerusalem who live on Mount Zion!


## Psalms 147:13 (id: 10019147013)

13He has made the defenses of your city strong, and he has blessed the people who live among you.


## Psalms 147:14 (id: 10019147014)

14He keeps your borders safe, and gives you plenty of wheat to eat.


## Psalms 147:15 (id: 10019147015)

15He gives commands about what will happen on the earth, and these things happen right away.


## Psalms 147:16 (id: 10019147016)

16He makes snow that covers the ground like a white wool blanket, and he scatters frost on the ground like ashes.


## Psalms 147:17 (id: 10019147017)

17He hurls down hail like small stones. When he makes it cold, no one can stand outside.


## Psalms 147:18 (id: 10019147018)

18But then he gives a command, and the snow and ice melt. He sends a warm wind, and the water flows again.


## Psalms 147:19 (id: 10019147019)

19He told the descendants of Jacob how he wanted them to live. Yes, he gave decrees and laws to the Israelites.


## Psalms 147:20 (id: 10019147020)

20He has not done this for any other nation; they do not know his laws. Praise Yahweh!


## Psalms 148:1 (id: 10019148001)

1Praise Yahweh! You who are in heaven, praise Yahweh; yes, you who are in the highest places, praise him.


## Psalms 148:2 (id: 10019148002)

2All you angels, praise him; all you heavenly armies, praise him.


## Psalms 148:3 (id: 10019148003)

3Sun and moon, praise him; all you shining stars, praise him.


## Psalms 148:4 (id: 10019148004)

4You highest heavens, praise him, and you waters above the sky, praise him.


## Psalms 148:5 (id: 10019148005)

5Let all of these praise Yahweh, because he created them by his own command.


## Psalms 148:6 (id: 10019148006)

6He set them in their places permanently; {to do that,} he made a decree that will always be in effect.


## Psalms 148:7 (id: 10019148007)

7You who are on the earth, praise Yahweh! You great sea creatures and everything deep in the ocean,


## Psalms 148:8 (id: 10019148008)

8lightning and hail, snow and clouds, and strong winds that obey what he commands,


## Psalms 148:9 (id: 10019148009)

9all you mountains and hills, all you fruit trees and cedar trees,


## Psalms 148:10 (id: 10019148010)

10all you wild animals and livestock, all you creatures that crawl on the ground and all you birds that fly in the sky,


## Psalms 148:11 (id: 10019148011)

11you kings of the earth and all your peoples, you rulers and all you judges,


## Psalms 148:12 (id: 10019148012)

12you young men and young women, you old people and children,


## Psalms 148:13 (id: 10019148013)

13you should all praise Yahweh, because he alone is truly great. He is more glorious than everything on earth and in heaven.


## Psalms 148:14 (id: 10019148014)

14He has made his people strong, and other nations respect them. His people are the Israelite people, who are close to him. Praise Yahweh!


## Psalms 149:1 (id: 10019149001)

1Praise Yahweh! Sing a new song to Yahweh! Let his faithful people praise him when they gather together.


## Psalms 149:2 (id: 10019149002)

2You Israelite people, God made you. Be happy because of what he has done for you. You people of Jerusalem, rejoice because God is your king.


## Psalms 149:3 (id: 10019149003)

3Praise Yahweh by dancing. Play tambourines and harps to honor him.


## Psalms 149:4 (id: 10019149004)

4Do that because Yahweh loves his people; he honors poor people by rescuing them.


## Psalms 149:5 (id: 10019149005)

5God’s faithful people should celebrate because he has honored them. Even on their beds at night, they should praise him with joyful songs.


## Psalms 149:6 (id: 10019149006)

6They should also shout loudly to praise God when they go into battle holding swords that have two sharp edges.


## Psalms 149:7 (id: 10019149007)

7{They should be ready} to punish the people of other nations for the sins they have committed against God.


## Psalms 149:8 (id: 10019149008)

8They should bind their kings with chains and put iron shackles on their leaders.


## Psalms 149:9 (id: 10019149009)

9In that way, they will carry out the judgment that God has decreed against them. God will give his faithful people the honor {of doing that for him}. Praise Yahweh!


## Psalms 150:1 (id: 10019150001)

1Praise Yahweh! Praise God in his temple here on earth, and praise him in the heavens that he created by his power!


## Psalms 150:2 (id: 10019150002)

2Praise him for the mighty things he has done! Praise him because he is so very great!


## Psalms 150:3 (id: 10019150003)

3Praise him by blowing horns loudly! Praise him by playing lyres and harps!


## Psalms 150:4 (id: 10019150004)

4Praise him by beating tambourines and by dancing! Praise him by playing stringed instruments and flutes!


## Psalms 150:5 (id: 10019150005)

5Praise him by playing loud cymbals and cymbals that make high notes!


## Psalms 150:6 (id: 10019150006)

6Let everything that breathes praise Yahweh! Praise Yahweh!
